<a id="words-1-100"></a>
## Words 1 – 100

<a id="w-1"></a>
### 1. [the](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/the_1) *(definite article)*
- **UK:** `/ðiː/` · **US:** `/ðiː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/the/the__/the__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/the/the__/the__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सर्वोत्तम, वह, वही
- **Examples:**
  - There were three questions. The first two were relatively easy but the third one was hard.
  - There was an accident here yesterday. A car hit a tree and the driver was killed.
  - The heat was getting to be too much for me.
  - The nights are getting longer.

<a id="w-2"></a>
### 2. [be](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/be_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/biː/` · **US:** `/biː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/be_/be__g/be__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/be_/be__u/be__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** होना, रहना, लगना
- **Examples:**
  - + noun Today is Monday.
  - ‘Who is that?’ ‘It's my brother.’
  - She's a great beauty.
  - Susan is a doctor.

<a id="w-3"></a>
### 3. [and](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/and) *(conjunction)*
- **UK:** `/ænd/` · **US:** `/ænd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/and/and__/and__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/and/and__/and__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** और
- **Examples:**
  - bread and butter
  - a table, two chairs and a desk
  - Sue and I left early.
  - Do it slowly and carefully.

<a id="w-4"></a>
### 4. [of](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/of) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/ɒv/` · **US:** `/ʌv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/of_/of__g/of__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/of_/of__u/of__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** का
- **Examples:**
  - a friend of mine
  - the love of a mother for her child
  - the role of the teacher
  - Can't you throw out that old bike of Tommy's?

<a id="w-5"></a>
### 5. [a](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/a_1) *(indefinite article)*
- **UK:** `/eɪ/` · **US:** `/eɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/a__/a__gb/a__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/a__/a__us/a__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** एक, ए
- **Examples:**
  - a man/horse/unit
  - an aunt/egg/hour/X-ray
  - I can only carry two at a time.
  - There's a visitor for you.

<a id="w-6"></a>
### 6. [in](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/in_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪn/` · **US:** `/ɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/in_/in__g/in__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/in_/in__u/in__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अन्दर
- **Examples:**
  - She opened the door and went in.
  - The kids were playing by the river and one of them fell in.

<a id="w-7"></a>
### 7. [to](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/to_2) *(infinitive marker)*
- **UK:** `/tuː/` · **US:** `/tuː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/to_/to__g/to__gb_7.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/to_/to__u/to__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** में, तक, से
- **Examples:**
  - I set out to buy food.
  - I am going to tell you a story.
  - She was determined to do well.
  - His aim was to become president.

<a id="w-8"></a>
### 8. [have](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/have_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/hæv/` · **US:** `/hæv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hav/have_/have__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hav/have_/have__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** होना, मिलना, रखना
- **Examples:**
  - He had a new car and a boat.
  - Have you got a job yet?
  - I don't have that much money on me.
  - She's got a BA in English.

<a id="w-9"></a>
### 9. [it](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/it_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌaɪ ˈtiː/` · **US:** `/ˌaɪ ˈtiː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/i_t/i_t__/i_t__1_gb_1_abbr.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/i_t/i_t__/i_t__1_us_1_abbr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वह, यह/वह, वाक्यांश  प्रमुखता बताना
- **Examples:**
  - the increasing use of IT in all aspects of today's society
  - in IT I've worked in IT for 30 years.
  - IT professionals/staff/managers
  - the IT industry/sector

<a id="w-10"></a>
### 10. [I](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/i_1) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/aɪ/` · **US:** `/aɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/i_1/i_1_g/i_1_gb_1_abbr.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/i__/i__us/i__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** एक, मुझे, आई
- **Examples:**
  - I think I'd better go now.
  - When they asked me if I wanted the job, I said yes.
  - He and I are old friends.
  - I'm not going to fall, am I?

<a id="w-11"></a>
### 11. [that](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/that_4) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ðæt/` · **US:** `/ðæt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tha/that_/that__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tha/that_/that__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इतना
- **Examples:**
  - I can't walk that far (= as far as that).
  - If they are that close, they will be here in minutes.
  - If only it were that simple.

<a id="w-12"></a>
### 12. [for](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/for_1) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/fɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/fɔːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/for/for__/for__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/for/for__/for__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** के हिसाब से, तक, के लिये
- **Examples:**
  - There's a letter for you.
  - It's a book for children.
  - We got a new table for the dining room.
  - This is the place for me (= I like it very much).

<a id="w-13"></a>
### 13. [you](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/you) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/juː/` · **US:** `/juː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/y/you/you__/you__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/y/you/you__/you__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तुम, आप
- **Examples:**
  - You said you knew the way.
  - I thought she told you.
  - Can I sit next to you?
  - I want both of you to help.

<a id="w-14"></a>
### 14. [he](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/he_1) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/hiː/` · **US:** `/hiː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/he_/he__g/he__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/he_/he__u/he__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वह, उस
- **Examples:**
  - Everyone liked my father—he was the perfect gentleman.
  - He (= the man we are watching) went through that door.

<a id="w-15"></a>
### 15. [with](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/with) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/wɪθ/` · **US:** `/wɪθ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wit/with_/with__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wit/with_/with__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** से, के पास, के साथ
- **Examples:**
  - She lives with her parents.
  - I have a client with me right now.
  - a nice steak with a bottle of red wine

<a id="w-16"></a>
### 16. [on](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/on_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ɒn/` · **US:** `/ɑːn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/on_/on__g/on__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/on_/on__u/on__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगातार
- **Examples:**
  - Put your coat on.
  - I didn't have my glasses on.
  - What did she have on (= what was she wearing)?

<a id="w-17"></a>
### 17. [do](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/do1_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/duː/` · **US:** `/duː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/do_/do__g/do__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/do_/do__u/do__us_3_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** करना, होना, चलाना
- **Examples:**
  - What are you doing this evening?
  - We will do what we can to help.
  - Are you doing anything tomorrow evening?
  - The company ought to do something about the poor service.

<a id="w-18"></a>
### 18. [say](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/say_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/seɪ/` · **US:** `/seɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/say/say__/say__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/say/say__/say__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निकटता, वचन, उदाहरण
- **Examples:**
  - say (in something) We had no say in the decision to sell the company.
  - People want a greater say in local government.
  - say on something The judge has the final say on the sentence.

<a id="w-19"></a>
### 19. [this](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/this_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ðɪs/` · **US:** `/ðɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/thi/this_/this__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/thi/this_/this__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इस, यह, इसेसे
- **Examples:**
  - It's about this high (= as high as I am showing you with my hands).
  - I didn't think we'd get this far.
  - They never expected to be quite this successful.
  - I honestly can't remember when I last enjoyed a film this much.

<a id="w-20"></a>
### 20. [they](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/they) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/ðeɪ/` · **US:** `/ðeɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/the/they_/they__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/the/they_/they__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वे, वे लोग, वेए
- **Examples:**
  - ‘Where are John and Liz?’ ‘They went for a walk.’
  - They (= the things you are carrying) go on the bottom shelf.

<a id="w-21"></a>
### 21. [at](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/at) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/æt/` · **US:** `/æt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/at_/at__g/at__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/at_/at__u/at__us_3_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** में, पर, से
- **Examples:**
  - at the corner of the street
  - We changed at Crewe.
  - They arrived late at the airport.
  - At the roundabout take the third exit.

<a id="w-22"></a>
### 22. [but](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/but_1) *(conjunction)*
- **UK:** `/bʌt/` · **US:** `/bʌt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/but/but__/but__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/but/but__/but__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** परन्तु
- **Examples:**
  - I got it wrong. It wasn't the red one but the blue one.
  - His mother won't be there, but his father might.
  - It isn't that he lied exactly, but he does tend to exaggerate.

<a id="w-23"></a>
### 23. [we](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/we) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/wiː/` · **US:** `/wiː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/we_/we__g/we__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/we_/we__u/we__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हम, हमार्, मैं
- **Examples:**
  - We've moved to Atlanta.
  - We'd (= the company would) like to offer you the job.
  - Why don't we go and see it together?

<a id="w-24"></a>
### 24. [his](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/his_1) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/ɪz/` · **US:** `/ɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/his/his__/his__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/his/his__/his__us_3_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उसका
- **Examples:**
  - James has sold his car.
  - He broke his leg skiing.
  - Discuss the reasons the author gives for his or her opinion.

<a id="w-25"></a>
### 25. [from](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/from) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/frɒm/` · **US:** `/frɑːm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fro/from_/from__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fro/from_/from__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** से, के यहाँ से
- **Examples:**
  - She began to walk away from him.
  - Has the train from Bristol arrived?

<a id="w-26"></a>
### 26. [not](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/not) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/nɒt/` · **US:** `/nɑːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/not/not__/not__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/not/not__/not__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नहीं
- **Examples:**
  - She did not/didn’t see him.
  - It’s not/It isn’t raining.
  - It's cold, isn't it?
  - Don't you eat meat?

<a id="w-27"></a>
### 27. [by](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/by_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/baɪ/` · **US:** `/baɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/by_/by__g/by__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/by_/by__u/by__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** में, तक, पास
- **Examples:**
  - Just drive by. Don't stop.
  - He hurried by without speaking to me.
  - Excuse me, I can't get by.
  - Time goes by so quickly.

<a id="w-28"></a>
### 28. [she](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/she_1) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/ʃiː/` · **US:** `/ʃiː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/she/she__/she__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/she/she__/she__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वह, अऔरत, स्त्री
- **Examples:**
  - ‘What does your sister do?’ ‘She's a dentist.’
  - Doesn't she (= the woman we are looking at) look like Sue?
  - Think about a friend or family member. What kind of person is he or she?

<a id="w-29"></a>
### 29. [or](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/or_1) *(conjunction)*
- **UK:** `/ɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/ɔːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/or_/or__g/or__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/or_/or__u/or__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** या/अथवा
- **Examples:**
  - Is your sister older or younger than you?
  - Are you coming or not?
  - Is it a boy or a girl?

<a id="w-30"></a>
### 30. [as](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/as_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/æz/` · **US:** `/æz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/as_/as__g/as__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/as_/as__u/as__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जैसा, मानो, क्योंकि
- **Examples:**
  - You're as tall as your father.
  - He was as white as a sheet.
  - She doesn't play as well as her sister.
  - I haven't known him as long as you (= as you have known him).

<a id="w-31"></a>
### 31. [what](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/what) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/wɒt/` · **US:** `/wʌt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wha/what_/what__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wha/what_/what__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जो, जो कुछ, कितना
- **Examples:**
  - What is your name?
  - What (= what job) does he do?
  - What time is it?
  - What kind of music do you like?

<a id="w-32"></a>
### 32. [go](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/go_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡəʊ/` · **US:** `/ɡəʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/go_/go__g/go__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/go_/go__u/go__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बारी, उत्साह, गो
- **Examples:**
  - Whose go is it?
  - It's your go.
  - ‘How much is it to play?’ ‘It's 50p a go.’
  - go on something Can I have a go on your new bike?

<a id="w-33"></a>
### 33. [their](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/their) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/ðeə(r)/` · **US:** `/ðer/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/the/their/their__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/the/their/their__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अपना, उनका या इनका, उनक्
- **Examples:**
  - Their parties are always fun.
  - Which is their house?

<a id="w-34"></a>
### 34. [can](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/can2_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kæn/` · **US:** `/kæn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/can/can__/can__gb_7.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/can/can__/can__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बाल्टी, पीपा, नितम्ब
- **Examples:**
  - can of something a can of beans/beer/soda
  - a beer can
  - He opened the can and took a large swig.
  - an aluminium/tin can

<a id="w-35"></a>
### 35. [who](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/who_1) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/huː/` · **US:** `/huː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/who/who__/who__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/who/who__/who__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** किसने, जो, कौन
- **Examples:**
  - Who is that woman?
  - I wonder who that letter was from.
  - Who are you phoning?
  - Who's the money for?

<a id="w-36"></a>
### 36. [get](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/get) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɡet/` · **US:** `/ɡet/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/get/get__/get__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/get/get__/get__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** करना, होना, पाना
- **Examples:**
  - I got a call from Dave this morning.
  - What (= What presents) did you get for your birthday?
  - He gets (= earns) about $40 000 a year.
  - This room gets very little sunshine.

<a id="w-37"></a>
### 37. [if](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/if_1) *(conjunction)*
- **UK:** `/ɪf/` · **US:** `/ɪf/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/if_/if__g/if__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/if_/if__u/if__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मानो
- **Examples:**
  - If you see him, give him this note.
  - I'll only stay if you offer me more money.
  - If necessary I can come at once.
  - You can stay for the weekend if you like.

<a id="w-38"></a>
### 38. [would](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/would) *(modal verb)*
- **UK:** `/əd/` · **US:** `/əd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wou/would/would__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wou/would/would__us_6_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** काल्पनिक घटनाओं को प्रकट करने में, इच्छा प्रकट करना, भूतकाल को प्रकट करने के लिए
- **Examples:**
  - Would you like a sandwich?
  - Would you have dinner with me on Friday?

<a id="w-39"></a>
### 39. [her](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/her_2) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/hɜː(r)/` · **US:** `/hɜːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/her/her__/her__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/her/her__/her__us_3_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उसका[स्त्री], उसे, उस स्त्री को
- **Examples:**
  - Meg loves her job.
  - She broke her leg skiing.

<a id="w-40"></a>
### 40. [all](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/all_3) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ɔːl/` · **US:** `/ɔːl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/all/all__/all__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/all/all__/all__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पूर्ण रुप से
- **Examples:**
  - She was dressed all in white.
  - He lives all alone.
  - The coffee went all over my skirt.

<a id="w-41"></a>
### 41. [my](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/my) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/maɪ/` · **US:** `/maɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/my_/my__g/my__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/my_/my__u/my__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मेरा
- **Examples:**
  - Where's my passport?
  - My feet are cold.

<a id="w-42"></a>
### 42. [make](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/make_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/meɪk/` · **US:** `/meɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mak/make_/make__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mak/make_/make__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** गठन
- **Examples:**
  - make of something What make of car does he drive?
  - a Swiss make of watch
  - There are so many different makes to choose from.
  - We need to know the make, model and year of your car.

<a id="w-43"></a>
### 43. [about](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/about_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈbaʊt/` · **US:** `/əˈbaʊt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/abo/about/about__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/abo/about/about__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आस पास, लगभग, बहुत हो गया
- **Examples:**
  - It costs about $10.
  - They waited (for) about an hour.
  - He arrived (at) about ten.

<a id="w-44"></a>
### 44. [know](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/know_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/nəʊ/` · **US:** `/nəʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/k/kno/know_/know__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/k/kno/know_/know__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समझना, पहचानना, जानना
- **Examples:**
  - know something No one knows the answer.
  - I need to know your name.
  - The truth about what happened is not yet known.
  - All I know is that she used to work in a bank (= I have no other information about her).

<a id="w-45"></a>
### 45. [will](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/will_4) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/wɪl/` · **US:** `/wɪl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wil/will_/will__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wil/will_/will__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इच्छा, वसीयतनामा, अभिलाषा
- **Examples:**
  - to have a strong will
  - to have an iron will/a will of iron
  - Her decision to continue shows great strength of will.
  - The matter became a battle of wills between the king and the barons.

<a id="w-46"></a>
### 46. [up](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/up_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ʌp/` · **US:** `/ʌp/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/u/up_/up__g/up__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/u/up_/up__u/up__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जगा हुआ, प्रत्येक, उठाना/चढ़ाना
- **Examples:**
  - He jumped up from his chair.
  - Your mum said you were up here (= upstairs).
  - The sun was already up (= had risen) when they set off.
  - They live up in the mountains.

<a id="w-47"></a>
### 47. [one](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/one_1) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/wʌn/` · **US:** `/wʌn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/one/one__/one__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/one/one__/one__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अकेला, जो, एक
- **Examples:**
  - Do you want one or two?
  - There's only room for one person.
  - One more, please!
  - a one-bedroomed apartment

<a id="w-48"></a>
### 48. [time](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/time_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/taɪm/` · **US:** `/taɪm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tim/time_/time__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tim/time_/time__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अवसर, ताल, काल
- **Examples:**
  - The world exists in time and space.
  - The changing seasons mark the passage of time.
  - in time A visit to the museum will take you back in time to the 1930s.
  - At this point in time, it seems unlikely that he will write any more books.

<a id="w-49"></a>
### 49. [there](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/there_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ðeə(r)/` · **US:** `/ðer/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/the/there/there__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/the/there/there__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वहाँ पर
- **Examples:**

  - There are two people waiting outside.
  - Has there been an accident?

<a id="w-50"></a>
### 50. [year](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/year) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/jɜː(r)/` · **US:** `/jɪr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/y/yea/year_/year__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/y/yea/year_/year__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वितवर्ष, साल, संवत

  - next/last year

<a id="w-51"></a>
### 51. [so](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/so_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/səʊ/` · **US:** `/səʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/so_/so__g/so__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/so_/so__u/so__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-52"></a>
### 52. [think](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/think_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/θɪŋk/` · **US:** `/θɪŋk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/thi/think/think__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/thi/think/think__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विचार करना, समझना, कल्पना करना
- **Examples:**
  - think (that)… Do you really think (that) he'll win?

  - I didn't think people were allowed to park here.
  - Am I right in thinking that you used to live here?

### 53. [when](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/when_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/wen/` · **US:** `/wen/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/whe/when_/when__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/whe/when_/when__us_1_rr.mp3)

  - When can I see you?

### 59. [take](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/take_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/teɪk/` · **US:** `/teɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tak/take_/take__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tak/take_/take__us_1.mp3)

### 60. [out](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/out_1) *(adverb)*

  - out of something I got out of bed.
  - He opened the box and out jumped a frog.

- **Meaning:** से

### 70. [like](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/like_4) *(noun)*

### 71. [other](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/other_1) *(adjective)*

- **Meaning:** दुसरा, अन्य, दूसरा
- **Examples:**

  - Are there any other questions?
  - There seem to be no other survivors.
  - I can't see you now—some other time, maybe.

<a id="w-72"></a>
### 72. [how](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/how) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/haʊ/` · **US:** `/haʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/how/how__/how__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/how/how__/how__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कैसे, कितना
- **Examples:**
  - How does it work?
  - He did not know how he ought to behave.
  - I'll show you how to load the software.
  - ‘Her behaviour was very odd.’ ‘How so?’

### 73. [then](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/then_1) *(adverb)*

- **Meaning:** तत्पश्चात
- **Examples:**
  - Life was harder then because neither of us had a job.
  - Things were very different back then.
  - She grew up in Zimbabwe, or Rhodesia as it then was.
  - I saw them at Christmas but haven't heard a thing since then.

<a id="w-80"></a>
### 80. [way](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/way_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/weɪ/` · **US:** `/weɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/way/way__/way__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/way/way__/way__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-81"></a>
### 81. [look](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/look_2) *(noun)*

- **Examples:**
  - look at somebody/something Here, have a look at this.

<a id="w-82"></a>
### 82. [first](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/first_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/fɜːst/` · **US:** `/fɜːrst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fir/first/first__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fir/first/first__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सबसे पहले, के पहले
- **Examples:**

  - First I had to decide what to wear.
  - Who came first in the race (= who won)?
  - It plunged nose first into the river.

<a id="w-83"></a>
### 83. [also](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/also) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɔːlsəʊ/` · **US:** `/ˈɔːlsəʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/als/also_/also__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/als/also_/also__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** और, भी, इसके  साथ ही
- **Examples:**
  - She's fluent in French and German. She also speaks a little Italian.
  - rubella, also known as German measles
  - I didn't like it that much. Also, it was much too expensive.
  - Jake's father had also been a doctor (= both Jake and his father were doctors).

<a id="w-84"></a>
### 84. [new](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/new) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/njuː/` · **US:** `/nuː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/new/new__/new__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/new/new__/new__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अज्ञात, कच्चा, भिन्न
- **Examples:**
  - Have you read her new novel?
  - We retrain staff to use the new technology.
  - The company is trialling a new security system.
  - Have you seen Danielle's new baby?

<a id="w-85"></a>
### 85. [because](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/because) *(conjunction)*
- **UK:** `/bɪˈkɒz/` · **US:** `/bɪˈkɔːz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bec/becau/because__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bec/becau/because__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** क्योँकि
- **Examples:**
  - I did it because he told me to.
  - Just because I don't complain, people think I'm satisfied.
  - Because it was so late, he found it unacceptable to send them all away.

<a id="w-86"></a>
### 86. [day](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/day) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/deɪ/` · **US:** `/deɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/day/day__/day__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/day/day__/day__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दिन, दिवस
- **Examples:**
  - ‘What day is it today?’ ‘Monday.’
  - I go to the gym every day.
  - We spent five days in Paris.
  - I saw Tom three days ago.

<a id="w-87"></a>
### 87. [more](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/more_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/mɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/mɔːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mor/more_/more__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mor/more_/more__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अधिक, अत्यधिक, अधिकतर
- **Examples:**
  - She was far more intelligent than her sister.
  - He read the letter more carefully the second time.

<a id="w-88"></a>
### 88. [use](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/use_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/juːs/` · **US:** `/juːs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/u/use/use__/use__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/u/use/use__/use__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लाभ/प्रयोजन, फ़ायदा, प्रयोजन
- **Examples:**
  - A ban was imposed on the use of chemical weapons.
  - You can't justify the use of military force.
  - I'm not sure that this is the most valuable use of my time.
  - for… use These videos are intended for classroom use.

<a id="w-89"></a>
### 89. [no](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/no_2) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/nəʊ/` · **US:** `/nəʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/no_/no__g/no__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/no_/no__u/no__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कोई, नहीं, बिल्कुल नहीं
- **Examples:**
  - No student is to leave the room.
  - I have no meetings this morning.
  - There's no bread left.
  - No two days are the same.

<a id="w-90"></a>
### 90. [man](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/man_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/mæn/` · **US:** `/mæn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/man/man__/man__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/man/man__/man__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** व्यक्ति, नौकर, आदमी
- **Examples:**
  - a good-looking young man
  - the relationships between men and women
  - the men's team/game/race/doubles/singles/event
  - The board of directors is dominated by middle-aged white men.

<a id="w-91"></a>
### 91. [find](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/find_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/faɪnd/` · **US:** `/faɪnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fin/find_/find__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fin/find_/find__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पाना, प्राप्त करना, मिलना
- **Examples:**
  - find somebody/something Look what I've found!
  - We've found a great new restaurant near the office.
  - A man out walking his dog found the body in a ditch.
  - find somebody/something + adj. A whale was found washed up on the shore.

<a id="w-92"></a>
### 92. [here](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/here_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/hɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/hɪr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/her/here_/here__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/her/here_/here__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इस स्थान पर
- **Examples:**
  - I live here.
  - Put the box here.
  - Click here for more details.
  - Let's get out of here.

<a id="w-93"></a>
### 93. [thing](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/thing) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/θɪŋ/` · **US:** `/θɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/thi/thing/thing__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/thi/thing/thing__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समस्या, कार्य, गुण
- **Examples:**
  - Can you pass me that thing over there?
  - She's very fond of sweet things (= sweet foods).
  - He's just bought one of those exercise things.
  - Turn that thing off while I'm talking to you!

<a id="w-94"></a>
### 94. [give](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/give_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɡɪv/` · **US:** `/ɡɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/giv/give_/give__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/giv/give_/give__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** करना, दिखाना, स्वीकार करना
- **Examples:**
  - give something to somebody Give the letter to your mother when you've read it.
  - She gave her ticket to the woman at the check-in desk.
  - give somebody something Give your mother the letter.
  - They were all given a box to carry.

<a id="w-95"></a>
### 95. [many](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/many) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/ˈmeni/` · **US:** `/ˈmeni/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/man/many_/many__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/man/many_/many__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जितना, बहुत  से, कई
- **Examples:**
  - We don't have very many copies left.
  - You can't have one each. We haven't got many.
  - Many people feel that the law should be changed.
  - Many of those present disagreed.

<a id="w-96"></a>
### 96. [well](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/well_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/wel/` · **US:** `/wel/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wel/well_/well__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wel/well_/well__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सन्तोषजनक, निरोग
- **Examples:**
  - I don't feel very well.
  - Is she well enough to travel?
  - Get well soon! (= for example, on a card)
  - I'm better now, thank you.

<a id="w-97"></a>
### 97. [only](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/only_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈəʊnli/` · **US:** `/ˈəʊnli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/onl/only_/only__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/onl/only_/only__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अकेला, ही, केवल
- **Examples:**
  - She's their only daughter.
  - We were the only people there.
  - His only answer was a grunt.
  - I was not the only one with moist eyes at the end of the film.

<a id="w-98"></a>
### 98. [those](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/those)
- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/those)
- **Meaning:** जो, वे, इन

<a id="w-99"></a>
### 99. [tell](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/tell) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/tel/` · **US:** `/tel/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tel/tell_/tell__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tel/tell_/tell__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बताना, निर्देश देना, सूचित करना
- **Examples:**
  - tell something to somebody He told the news to everybody he saw.
  - tell somebody something He told everybody he saw the news.
  - Did she tell you her name?
  - What did I tell you? (= you should have listened to my advice)

<a id="w-100"></a>
### 100. [one](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/one_1) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/wʌn/` · **US:** `/wʌn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/one/one__/one__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/one/one__/one__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अकेला, जो, एक
- **Examples:**
  - Do you want one or two?
  - There's only room for one person.
  - One more, please!
  - a one-bedroomed apartment

<a id="words-101-200"></a>
## Words 101 – 200

<a id="w-101"></a>
### 101. [very](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/very_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈveri/` · **US:** `/ˈveri/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/v/ver/very_/very__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/v/ver/very_/very__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ठीक, बहुत, अत्यन्त
- **Examples:**
  - He might be phoning her at this very moment.
  - The US initiated the deal for that very reason.
  - I'd intended to tell him that very day.
  - Those were her very words.

<a id="w-102"></a>
### 102. [even](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/even_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈiːvn/` · **US:** `/ˈiːvn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eve/even_/even__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eve/even_/even__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** एक सा, भी, बराबर
- **Examples:**
  - 4, 6, 8 and 10 are all even numbers.

<a id="w-103"></a>
### 103. [back](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/back_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/bæk/` · **US:** `/bæk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bac/back_/back__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bac/back_/back__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पीछे का
- **Examples:**
  - We were sitting in the back row
  - the back garden (= behind the house)
  - a back room (= one at the back of a building)
  - My phone's in my back pocket.

<a id="w-104"></a>
### 104. [any](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/any_3) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈeni/` · **US:** `/ˈeni/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/any/any__/any__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/any/any__/any__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रत्येक, थोड़ा, एक
- **Examples:**
  - He wasn't any good at French.
  - I can't run any faster.
  - Is your father feeling any better?
  - I don't want any more.

<a id="w-105"></a>
### 105. [good](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/good_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɡʊd/` · **US:** `/ɡʊd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/goo/good_/good__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/goo/good_/good__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अच्छा
- **Examples:**
  - a good book
  - good food
  - The piano was in really good condition.
  - Your work is just not good enough.

<a id="w-106"></a>
### 106. [woman](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/woman) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈwʊmən/` · **US:** `/ˈwʊmən/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wom/woman/woman__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wom/woman/woman__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रेमिका, महिला, पत्नी
- **Examples:**
  - a 54-year-old woman
  - an interesting young woman
  - married/single women
  - men, women and children

<a id="w-107"></a>
### 107. [through](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/through_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/θruː/` · **US:** `/θruː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/thr/throu/through__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/thr/throu/through__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** में, सीधा, पूरी तरह से
- **Examples:**
  - Put the coffee in the filter and let the water run through.
  - The tyre's flat—the nail has gone right through.
  - The onlookers stood aside to let the paramedics through.

<a id="w-108"></a>
### 108. [us](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/us_1) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/ʌs/` · **US:** `/ʌs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/u/us_/us__g/us__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/u/us_/us__u/us__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** यू एस, हमें/हम को, हमारे
- **Examples:**
  - She gave us a picture as a wedding present.
  - We'll take the dog with us.
  - Hello, it's us back again.

<a id="w-109"></a>
### 109. [life](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/life_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/laɪf/` · **US:** `/laɪf/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lif/life_/life__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lif/life_/life__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जीवन, प्राणी, सजीव
- **Examples:**
  - This could mean the difference between life and death.
  - The body was cold and showed no signs of life.
  - I wish we could bring dinosaurs back to life.
  - In spring the countryside bursts into life.

<a id="w-110"></a>
### 110. [child](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/child) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/tʃaɪld/` · **US:** `/tʃaɪld/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/chi/child/child__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/chi/child/child__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नासमझ, बच्चा, लाल
- **Examples:**
  - a child of 3/a 3-year-old child
  - men, women and children
  - an organization that campaigns for the rights of the unborn child
  - The film is not suitable for young children.

<a id="w-111"></a>
### 111. [work](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/work_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/wɜːk/` · **US:** `/wɜːrk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wor/work_/work__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wor/work_/work__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लेखन, आजीविका, प्रबंध
- **Examples:**
  - He started work as a security guard.
  - I'm still looking for work.
  - It is difficult to find work in the present economic climate.
  - She's planning to return to work once the children start school.

<a id="w-112"></a>
### 112. [down](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/down_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/daʊn/` · **US:** `/daʊn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dow/down_/down__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dow/down_/down__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** गिरा देना, पर, जल्दी समाप्त करना
- **Examples:**
  - She jumped down off the chair.
  - He looked down at her.
  - We watched as the sun went down.
  - She bent down to pick up her glove.

<a id="w-113"></a>
### 113. [may](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/may_3) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/meɪ/` · **US:** `/meɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/may/may_1/may_1_gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/may/may__/may__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संभवतः, मई/वसन्त, मईई
- **Examples:**
  - She was born in May.
  - (British English) The meeting is on the fifth of May/May the fifth.
  - (North American English) The meeting is on May fifth.
  - We went to Japan last May.

<a id="w-114"></a>
### 114. [after](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/after_3) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɑːftə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈæftər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/aft/after/after__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/aft/after/after__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बाद में, पीछे
- **Examples:**
  - That was in 1996. Soon after, I heard that he'd died.
  - I could come next week, or the week after.
  - And they all lived happily ever after.

<a id="w-115"></a>
### 115. [should](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/should) *(modal verb)*
- **UK:** `/ʃʊd/` · **US:** `/ʃʊd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sho/shoul/should__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sho/shoul/should__us_4_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** चाहिये{करना}, चाहिएएना, होताना
- **Examples:**
  - You shouldn't drink and drive.
  - He should have been more careful.
  - A present for me? You shouldn't have! (= used to thank somebody politely)

<a id="w-116"></a>
### 116. [call](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/call_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kɔːl/` · **US:** `/kɔːl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cal/call_/call__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cal/call_/call__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अवसर, संकेत, काल
- **Examples:**
  - to get/receive a call from somebody
  - to give somebody/make a call
  - I'll take (= answer) the call upstairs.
  - I left a message but he didn't return my call.

<a id="w-117"></a>
### 117. [world](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/world) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/wɜːld/` · **US:** `/wɜːrld/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wor/world/world__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wor/world/world__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वर्ग, युग, लोग
- **Examples:**
  - a map of the world
  - French is spoken in many parts of the world.
  - in the world Which is the largest city in the world?
  - around/round the world to sail around the world

<a id="w-118"></a>
### 118. [over](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/over_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈəʊvə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈəʊvər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/ove/over_/over__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/ove/over_/over__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तक, पार करना, खतम
- **Examples:**
  - I stopped and crossed over.
  - He rowed us over to the other side of the lake.
  - They have gone over to France.
  - This is my aunt who's over from Canada.

<a id="w-119"></a>
### 119. [school](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/school_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/skuːl/` · **US:** `/skuːl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sch/schoo/school__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sch/schoo/school__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समूह, शिक्षा, शाखा
- **Examples:**
  - My brother and I went to the same school.
  - (formal) Which school do they attend?
  - I'm going to the school today to talk to Kim's teacher.
  - We need more money for roads, hospitals and schools.

<a id="w-120"></a>
### 120. [still](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/still_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/stɪl/` · **US:** `/stɪl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sti/still/still__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sti/still/still__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शान्त, अचल/स्थिर
- **Examples:**
  - A fallen tree floated in the still water.
  - Keep still while I brush your hair.
  - The kids found it hard to stay still.
  - Can't you sit still?

<a id="w-121"></a>
### 121. [try](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/try_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/traɪ/` · **US:** `/traɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/try/try__/try__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/try/try__/try__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रयास, ^, जाँच
- **Examples:**
  - I doubt they'll be able to help but it's worth a try (= worth asking them).
  - try at doing something Why don't you have a try at convincing him?
  - (North American English) The US negotiators decided to make another try at reaching a settlement.
  - try at something This is my first try at fiction.

<a id="w-122"></a>
### 122. [in](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/in_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪn/` · **US:** `/ɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/in_/in__g/in__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/in_/in__u/in__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अन्दर
- **Examples:**
  - She opened the door and went in.
  - The kids were playing by the river and one of them fell in.

<a id="w-123"></a>
### 123. [as](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/as_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/æz/` · **US:** `/æz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/as_/as__g/as__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/as_/as__u/as__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जैसा, मानो, क्योंकि
- **Examples:**
  - You're as tall as your father.
  - He was as white as a sheet.
  - She doesn't play as well as her sister.
  - I haven't known him as long as you (= as you have known him).

<a id="w-124"></a>
### 124. [last](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/last1_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/lɑːst/` · **US:** `/læst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/las/last_/last__gb_6.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/las/last_/last__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** चलना, जीवित रहना, ठहरना
- **Examples:**
  - He came last in the race.
  - They arrived last of all.

<a id="w-125"></a>
### 125. [ask](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ask_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɑːsk/` · **US:** `/æsk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ask/ask__/ask__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ask/ask__/ask__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बुलाना, पूछना, आवश्यकता होना
- **Examples:**
  - How old are you—if you don’t mind me/my asking?
  - ask about somebody/something He asked about her family.
  - ask something Can I ask a question?
  - Did you ask the price?

<a id="w-126"></a>
### 126. [need](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/need_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/niːd/` · **US:** `/niːd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nee/need_/need__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nee/need_/need__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रयोजन, आवश्यकता, निर्धनता
- **Examples:**
  - to satisfy/meet/fulfil a need
  - need for something There is an urgent need for qualified teachers.
  - a desperate/pressing need for something
  - They identified a need for further training.

<a id="w-127"></a>
### 127. [too](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/too) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/tuː/` · **US:** `/tuː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/too/too__/too__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/too/too__/too__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अधिक, बहुत अधिक, आवश्यकता से अधिक
- **Examples:**
  - He's far too young to go on his own.
  - She was much too big to be carried.
  - This is too large a helping for me/This helping is too large for me.
  - Is it too much to ask for a little quiet?

<a id="w-128"></a>
### 128. [feel](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/feel_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/fiːl/` · **US:** `/fiːl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fee/feel_/feel__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fee/feel_/feel__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्पर्श, अनुभव
- **Examples:**
  - You can tell it's silk by the feel.
  - The model has the look and feel of wood.
  - She loved the feel of the sun on her skin.

<a id="w-129"></a>
### 129. [three](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/three) *(number)*
- **UK:** `/θriː/` · **US:** `/θriː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/thr/three/three__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/thr/three/three__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तीन, तिक्की
- **Examples:**
  - There are only three cookies left.
  - three of Sweden’s top financial experts
  - Ten people were invited but only three turned up.
  - Can you lend me three dollars?

<a id="w-130"></a>
### 130. [state](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/state_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/steɪt/` · **US:** `/steɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sta/state/state__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sta/state/state__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बताना, व्यक्त करना, अभिव्यक्त करना
- **Examples:**
  - state officials/agencies
  - state education
  - families dependent on state benefits (= in Britain, money given by the government to people who are poor)
  - state secrets (= information that could be harmful to a country if it were discovered by an enemy)

<a id="w-131"></a>
### 131. [never](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/never_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnevə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈnevər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nev/never/never__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nev/never/never__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कभी नहीं, बिल्कुल नहीं, कभी भी
- **Examples:**
  - You never help me.
  - He'll never forget her.
  - He has never been abroad.
  - She had never seen so much snow.

<a id="w-132"></a>
### 132. [become](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/become) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/bɪˈkʌm/` · **US:** `/bɪˈkʌm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bec/becom/become__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bec/becom/become__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** होना, उचित होना, बनना
- **Examples:**
  - + adj. It soon became apparent that no one was going to come.
  - It is becoming increasingly clear that something has gone seriously wrong.
  - When did you first become aware of the problem?
  - It was becoming more and more difficult to live on his salary.

<a id="w-133"></a>
### 133. [between](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/between_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/bɪˈtwiːn/` · **US:** `/bɪˈtwiːn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bet/betwe/between__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bet/betwe/between__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बीच, के बीच में, बीच में
- **Examples:**
  - The house was near a park but there was a road in between.
  - I see her most weekends but not very often in between.

<a id="w-134"></a>
### 134. [high](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/high_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/haɪ/` · **US:** `/haɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hig/high_/high__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hig/high_/high__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पद में ऊँचा, बुलंद
- **Examples:**
  - What's the highest mountain in the US?
  - The house has a high wall all the way round it.
  - shoes with high heels
  - He has a round face with a high forehead.

<a id="w-135"></a>
### 135. [really](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/really) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈrɪəli/` · **US:** `/ˈriːəli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rea/reall/really__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rea/reall/really__us_3_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सचमुच, आश्चर्य प्रकट करना, वस्तुतः
- **Examples:**
  - That's a really good idea.
  - It's really hard to find a decent job these days.
  - It all went really well.
  - This is a really nice place.

<a id="w-136"></a>
### 136. [something](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/something_1) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsʌmθɪŋ/` · **US:** `/ˈsʌmθɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/som/somet/something__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/som/somet/something__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कोई, कुछ, कोई  चीज
- **Examples:**
  - We stopped for something to eat.
  - Give me something to do.
  - There's something wrong with the TV.
  - There's something about this place that frightens me.

<a id="w-137"></a>
### 137. [most](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/most_3) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/məʊst/` · **US:** `/məʊst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mos/most_/most__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mos/most_/most__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगभग, अत्यधिक, बहुत अधिक
- **Examples:**
  - the most boring/beautiful part
  - It was the people with the least money who gave most generously.

<a id="w-138"></a>
### 138. [another](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/another) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/əˈnʌðə(r)/` · **US:** `/əˈnʌðər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ano/anoth/another__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ano/anoth/another__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भिन्न, दूसरा, एक अन्य
- **Examples:**
  - Would you like another drink?
  - ‘Finished?’ ‘No, I've got another three questions to do.’
  - We've still got another (= a further) forty miles to go.
  - ‘It's a bill.’ ‘Oh no, not another!’

<a id="w-139"></a>
### 139. [much](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/much_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/mʌtʃ/` · **US:** `/mʌtʃ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/muc/much_/much__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/muc/much_/much__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अधिक, बहुत, और
- **Examples:**
  - Thank you very much for the flowers.
  - I would very much like to see you again.
  - He isn't in the office much (= often).
  - You worry too much.

<a id="w-140"></a>
### 140. [family](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/family_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfæməli/` · **US:** `/ˈfæməli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fam/famil/family__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fam/famil/family__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कुल, वर्ग  का उपविभाग, वर्ग
- **Examples:**
  - family life
  - The job was starting to affect his family life.
  - your family background
  - Only close family members called her ‘Sally’.

<a id="w-141"></a>
### 141. [own](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/own_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əʊn/` · **US:** `/əʊn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/own/own__/own__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/own/own__/own__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्वयं, स्वीकार करना, अपना
- **Examples:**
  - It was her own idea.
  - I saw it with my own eyes (= I didn't hear about it from somebody else).
  - Is the car your own?
  - Your day off is your own (= you can spend it as you wish).

<a id="w-142"></a>
### 142. [leave](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/leave_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/liːv/` · **US:** `/liːv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lea/leave/leave__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lea/leave/leave__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बिदाई, छुट्टी, इजाजत.
- **Examples:**
  - to take a month’s paid/unpaid leave
  - How much annual leave do you get?
  - on leave soldiers home on leave
  - She's on maternity leave.

<a id="w-143"></a>
### 143. [put](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/put) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/pʊt/` · **US:** `/pʊt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/put/put__/put__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/put/put__/put__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अधिस्ठित करना, प्रकट करना, लगाना
- **Examples:**
  - Put the cases down there, please.
  - Did you put sugar in my coffee?
  - Put your hand up if you need more paper.

<a id="w-144"></a>
### 144. [old](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/old) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əʊld/` · **US:** `/əʊld/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/old/old__/old__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/old/old__/old__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सड़ा हुआ, पुराना, अनुभवी
- **Examples:**
  - be… years, months, etc. old The baby was only a few hours old.
  - In those days most people left school when they were only fifteen years old.
  - At thirty years old, he was already earning £40 000 a year.
  - two fourteen-year-old boys

<a id="w-145"></a>
### 145. [while](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/while_1) *(conjunction)*
- **UK:** `/waɪl/` · **US:** `/waɪl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/whi/while/while__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/whi/while/while__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जब कि, उस अवधि में
- **Examples:**
  - We must have been burgled while we were asleep.
  - Her parents died while she was still at school.
  - While I was waiting at the bus stop, three buses went by in the opposite direction.
  - You can go swimming while I'm having lunch.

<a id="w-146"></a>
### 146. [mean](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/mean_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/miːn/` · **US:** `/miːn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mea/mean_/mean__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mea/mean_/mean__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** होना, चाहना, कमीना
- **Examples:**
  - mean something What does this sentence mean?
  - What does ‘lark’ mean?
  - The word ‘Kuching’ means ‘cat’ in Malay.
  - Many languages use a single word to mean both music and dance.

<a id="w-147"></a>
### 147. [on](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/on_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ɒn/` · **US:** `/ɑːn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/on_/on__g/on__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/on_/on__u/on__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगातार
- **Examples:**
  - Put your coat on.
  - I didn't have my glasses on.
  - What did she have on (= what was she wearing)?

<a id="w-148"></a>
### 148. [keep](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/keep_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kiːp/` · **US:** `/kiːp/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/k/kee/keep_/keep__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/k/kee/keep_/keep__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पालन करना, रोकना, चलाना
- **Examples:**
  - He kept all her letters.
  - She handed the waiter a hundred dollar bill and told him to keep the change.
  - Always keep a backup of the file.

<a id="w-150"></a>
### 150. [why](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/why_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/waɪ/` · **US:** `/waɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/why/why__/why__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/why/why__/why__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** किसलिये, क्यों, किसलिए
- **Examples:**
  - Why were you late?
  - Tell me why you did it.
  - ‘I would like you to go.’ ‘Why me?’
  - (informal) Why oh why do people keep leaving the door open?

<a id="w-151"></a>
### 151. [let](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/let_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/let/` · **US:** `/let/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/let/let__/let__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/let/let__/let__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कम करना, देना, निकलना
- **Examples:**
  - ‘Shall we check it again?’ ‘Yes, let's.’
  - let's do something Let's go to the beach.
  - Right, let's begin.
  - (formal) Let us review these points in more detail.

<a id="w-152"></a>
### 152. [great](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/great_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɡreɪt/` · **US:** `/ɡreɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gre/great/great__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gre/great/great__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भारी, प्रमुख, शानदार
- **Examples:**
  - He's a great bloke.
  - What a great goal!
  - We had a great time in Madrid.
  - You should have come along. It was great fun.

<a id="w-153"></a>
### 153. [same](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/same_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/seɪm/` · **US:** `/seɪm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sam/same_/same__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sam/same_/same__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समान/सदृश
- **Examples:**
  - We have lived in the same house for twenty years.
  - Our children go to the same school as theirs.
  - She's still the same fun-loving person that I knew at college.
  - This one works in exactly the same way as the other.

<a id="w-154"></a>
### 154. [big](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/big_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/bɪɡ/` · **US:** `/bɪɡ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/big/big__/big__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/big/big__/big__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-155"></a>
### 155. [group](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/group_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡruːp/` · **US:** `/ɡruːp/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gro/group/group__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gro/group/group__us_1.mp3)

### 156. [begin](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/begin) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/bɪˈɡɪn/` · **US:** `/bɪˈɡɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/beg/begin/begin__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/beg/begin/begin__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - begin by doing something She began by thanking us all for coming.

<a id="w-157"></a>
### 157. [seem](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/seem) *(linking verb)*
- **UK:** `/siːm/` · **US:** `/siːm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/see/seem_/seem__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/see/seem_/seem__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-158"></a>
### 158. [country](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/country) *(noun)*

### 159. [help](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/help_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/help/` · **US:** `/help/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hel/help_/help__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hel/help_/help__us_1.mp3)

### 160. [talk](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/talk_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/tɔːk/` · **US:** `/tɔːk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tal/talk_/talk__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tal/talk_/talk__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** चर्चा, बोली, वार्ता
- **Examples:**

<a id="w-161"></a>
### 161. [where](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/where_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/weə(r)/` · **US:** `/wer/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/whe/where/where__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/whe/where/where__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जिस स्थान पर, जब कि, जहाँ
- **Examples:**
  - Where do you live?

  - Where (= in what book, newspaper, etc.) did you read that?

<a id="w-162"></a>
### 162. [turn](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/turn_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/tɜːn/` · **US:** `/tɜːrn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tur/turn_/turn__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tur/turn_/turn__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रदर्शन, परी, परिवर्तन
- **Examples:**
  - When it's your turn, take another card.
  - Please wait your turn.
  - somebody's turn to do something Whose turn is it to cook?
  - Steve took a turn driving while I slept.

<a id="w-163"></a>
### 163. [problem](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/problem_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈprɒbləm/` · **US:** `/ˈprɑːbləm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pro/probl/problem__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pro/probl/problem__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समस्या, परेशानी, प्रश्न
- **Examples:**
  - big/serious/major problems

  - financial/social/technical problems
  - Let me know if you have any problems.

<a id="w-164"></a>
### 164. [every](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/every) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/ˈevri/` · **US:** `/ˈevri/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eve/every/every__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eve/every/every__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रत्येक, हर एक, हर
- **Examples:**
  - She knows every student in the school.
  - I could hear every word they said.
  - We enjoyed every minute of our stay.
  - I go to the gym every day.

<a id="w-165"></a>
### 165. [start](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/start_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/stɑːt/` · **US:** `/stɑːrt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sta/start/start__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sta/start/start__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मौका, प्रारंभ, मदद
- **Examples:**
  - If we don't hurry, we'll miss the start.
  - They haven't won since the start of the season.
  - at the start (of something) Things didn't look too hopeful at the start of the year.
  - from the start We've had problems right from the start.

<a id="w-166"></a>
### 166. [hand](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hand_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/hænd/` · **US:** `/hænd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/han/hand_/hand__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/han/hand_/hand__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हन, कर्मचारी, गुच्छा
- **Examples:**
  - Ian placed a hand on her shoulder.
  - Keep both hands on the steering wheel at all times.
  - She had a large diamond on the ring finger of her left hand.
  - in your hand He was holding a large mug of coffee in his right hand.

<a id="w-167"></a>
### 167. [might](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/might_1) *(modal verb)*
- **UK:** `/maɪt/` · **US:** `/maɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mig/might/might__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mig/might/might__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सकना, सकेग्ना, हो  सकना
- **Examples:**
  - He might get there in time, but I can't be sure.
  - It might not be too late to save the building.
  - I know Vicky doesn't like the job, but I mightn't find it too bad.
  - The pills might have helped him, if only he'd taken them regularly.
