  - a quiz show

<a id="w-170"></a>
### 170. [part](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/part_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pɑːt/` · **US:** `/pɑːrt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/par/part_/part__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/par/part_/part__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हिस्सा, काल, क्षेत्र
- **Examples:**
  - We spent part of the time in the museum.
  - Part of the building was destroyed in the fire.
  - Part of the problem is that more and more people live alone.
  - Voters are given only part of the story (= only some of the information).

<a id="w-171"></a>
### 171. [about](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/about_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈbaʊt/` · **US:** `/əˈbaʊt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/abo/about/about__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/abo/about/about__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आस पास, लगभग, बहुत हो गया
- **Examples:**
  - It costs about $10.
  - They waited (for) about an hour.
  - He arrived (at) about ten.

<a id="w-172"></a>
### 172. [against](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/against) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/əˈɡeɪnst/` · **US:** `/əˈɡeɪnst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/aga/again/against__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/aga/again/against__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** से अलग, के सहारे, विरूद्व
- **Examples:**
  - the fight against terrorism
  - We're playing against the league champions next week.
  - We were rowing against the current.
  - That's against the law.

<a id="w-173"></a>
### 173. [place](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/place_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pleɪs/` · **US:** `/pleɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pla/place/place__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pla/place/place__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अवसर, कार्य, पद
- **Examples:**
  - in a place Keep your purse in a safe place.
  - I can't be in two places at once.
  - They organized several demonstrations in public places.
  - I happened to be in the right place at the right time.

<a id="w-174"></a>
### 174. [over](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/over_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈəʊvə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈəʊvər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/ove/over_/over__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/ove/over_/over__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तक, पार करना, खतम
- **Examples:**
  - I stopped and crossed over.
  - He rowed us over to the other side of the lake.
  - They have gone over to France.
  - This is my aunt who's over from Canada.

<a id="w-175"></a>
### 175. [such](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/such) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/sʌtʃ/` · **US:** `/sʌtʃ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/suc/such_/such__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/suc/such_/such__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इस तरह का, वैसा ही, इस
- **Examples:**
  - This issue was of such importance that we could not afford to ignore it.
  - such a/an… Why are you in such a hurry?
  - It's such a beautiful day!
  - such is something that… (formal) Such is the elegance of this typeface that it is still a favourite of designers.

<a id="w-176"></a>
### 176. [again](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/again) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈɡeɪn/` · **US:** `/əˈɡeɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/aga/again/again__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/aga/again/again__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लेकिन फिर, एक बार फिर, फिर  से
- **Examples:**
  - This must never happen again.
  - Try again—it takes practice.
  - Can we start again, please?
  - Could you say it again, please?

<a id="w-177"></a>
### 177. [few](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/few_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/fjuː/` · **US:** `/fjuː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/few/few__/few__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/few/few__/few__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अल्प, थोड़ा, कई
- **Examples:**
  - We've had a few replies.
  - I need a few things from the store.
  - Quite a few people are going to arrive early.
  - I try to visit my parents every few weeks.

<a id="w-178"></a>
### 178. [case](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/case_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/keɪs/` · **US:** `/keɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cas/case_/case__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cas/case_/case__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समस्या, खोल, उदाहरण
- **Examples:**
  - in… cases In some cases people have had to wait several weeks for an appointment.
  - In most cases the increases have been marginal.
  - In many cases it is the pressure of the job that causes managers to behave like this.
  - What should be done in such cases?

<a id="w-179"></a>
### 179. [most](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/most_3) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/məʊst/` · **US:** `/məʊst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mos/most_/most__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mos/most_/most__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगभग, अत्यधिक, बहुत अधिक
- **Examples:**
  - the most boring/beautiful part
  - It was the people with the least money who gave most generously.

<a id="w-180"></a>
### 180. [week](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/week) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/wiːk/` · **US:** `/wiːk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wee/week_/week__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wee/week_/week__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सप्ताह
- **Examples:**
  - last/this/next week
  - They won by two goals the previous week.
  - He was returning to Ethiopia the following week.
  - It rained all week.

<a id="w-201"></a>
### 201. [night](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/night) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/naɪt/` · **US:** `/naɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nig/night/night__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nig/night/night__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** रात, रजनी
- **Examples:**
  - at night These animals only come out at night.
  - by night They sleep by day and hunt by night.
  - in the night Does your baby still wake in the night?
  - She woke up in the middle of the night.

<a id="w-202"></a>
### 202. [live](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/live2_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/laɪv/` · **US:** `/laɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/liv/live_/live__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/liv/live_/live__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जीवित, घटित होता हुआ, जीवन्त
- **Examples:**
  - live animals
  - the number of live births (= babies born alive)
  - We saw a real live rattlesnake!
  - I need to talk to a live person (= not a recording or computer).

<a id="w-204"></a>
### 204. [point](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/point_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pɔɪnt/` · **US:** `/pɔɪnt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/poi/point/point__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/poi/point/point__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पइंट पर तैनात खिलाड़ी, विशेषता, द्विग्बिंदु
- **Examples:**
  - She made several interesting points in the article.
  - I take your point (= understand and accept what you are saying).
  - He's just saying that to prove a point (= to show his idea is right).
  - OK, you've made your point!

<a id="w-205"></a>
### 205. [believe](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/believe) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/bɪˈliːv/` · **US:** `/bɪˈliːv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bel/belie/believe__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bel/belie/believe__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समझना, भरोसा  करना, मानना
- **Examples:**
  - believe somebody I don't believe you!
  - The man claimed to be a social worker and she believed him.
  - Do you really believe her?
  - Believe me, she's not right for you.

<a id="w-206"></a>
### 206. [hold](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hold_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/həʊld/` · **US:** `/həʊld/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hol/hold_/hold__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hol/hold_/hold__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पकड़, खाव
- **Examples:**
  - hold on somebody/something His hold on her arm tightened.
  - He lost his hold on the rock and was swept away by the tide.
  - Make sure you've got a steady hold on the camera.
  - She tried to keep hold of the child's hand.

<a id="w-207"></a>
### 207. [today](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/today_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/təˈdeɪ/` · **US:** `/təˈdeɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tod/today/today__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tod/today/today__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आज
- **Examples:**
  - I've got a piano lesson later today.
  - The exams start a week today/today week (= one week from now).
  - The company today announced a plan that involves cutting 12% of its workforce.
  - A press release is due out today or tomorrow.

<a id="w-208"></a>
### 208. [bring](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/bring) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/brɪŋ/` · **US:** `/brɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bri/bring/bring__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bri/bring/bring__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगाना, उत्तेजित करना, लाना
- **Examples:**
  - bring somebody/something (with you) Don't forget to bring your books with you.
  - Can we bring the children?
  - bring somebody/something to something She brought her boyfriend to the party.
  - bring something for somebody Bring a present for Helen.

<a id="w-209"></a>
### 209. [happen](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/happen) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈhæpən/` · **US:** `/ˈhæpən/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hap/happe/happen__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hap/happe/happen__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** होना, घटित होना, कर  डिख्ना
- **Examples:**
  - You'll never guess what's happened!
  - Accidents like this happen all the time.
  - The most recent incident happened only a week ago.
  - The government has denied that the events ever happened.

<a id="w-210"></a>
### 210. [next](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/next_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/nekst/` · **US:** `/nekst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nex/next_/next__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nex/next_/next__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पास का, आने वाला, आगामी
- **Examples:**
  - The next train to Baltimore is at ten.
  - The next six months will be the hardest.
  - The next chapter deals with the post-war situation.
  - Who's next?

<a id="w-211"></a>
### 211. [without](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/without_1) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/wɪˈðaʊt/` · **US:** `/wɪˈðaʊt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wit/witho/without__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wit/witho/without__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** के बिना
- **Examples:**
  - They had gone two days without food.
  - He found the place without difficulty.
  - She spoke without much enthusiasm.

<a id="w-212"></a>
### 212. [before](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/before_3) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/bɪˈfɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/bɪˈfɔːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bef/befor/before__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bef/befor/before__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आगे, पहले से, पहले
- **Examples:**
  - You should have told me so before.
  - It had been fine the week before (= the previous week).
  - That had happened long before (= a long time earlier).
  - I think we've met before.

<a id="w-213"></a>
### 213. [large](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/large_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/lɑːdʒ/` · **US:** `/lɑːrdʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lar/large/large__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lar/large/large__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** व्यापक, विशाल
- **Examples:**
  - a large group/city/area/crowd/family

  - There were some very large amounts of money involved.
  - Drought hit large parts of the country.

- **UK:** `/blæk/` · **US:** `/blæk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bla/black/black__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bla/black/black__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भयंकर, श्याम, काला/श्याम/

  - black storm clouds
  - She had curly black hair and hazel eyes.

<a id="w-247"></a>
### 247. [long](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/long_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/lɒŋ/` · **US:** `/lɔːŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lon/long_/long__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lon/long_/long__us_8.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लम्बा, चिर

<a id="w-248"></a>
### 248. [both](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/both) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/bəʊθ/` · **US:** `/bəʊθ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bot/both_/both__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bot/both_/both__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दोनो/उभय, दोनों
- **Examples:**
  - Both women were French.
  - Both the women were French.
  - Both of the women were French.

<a id="w-249"></a>
### 249. [little](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/little_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈlɪtl/` · **US:** `/ˈlɪtl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lit/littl/little__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lit/littl/little__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** छोटा, महत्वहीन, थोडा

  - a little old lady

<a id="w-250"></a>
### 250. [house](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/house_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/haʊs/` · **US:** `/haʊs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hou/house/house__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hou/house/house__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** घर, प्रतिष्ठान, सदन
- **Examples:**
  - in a house We live in a two-bedroom house.
  - in the house It was so hot outside we stayed in the house.
  - into the house He went into the house.
  - out of the house I had to get out of the house.

<a id="w-251"></a>
### 251. [yes](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/yes_1) *(exclamation)*
- **UK:** `/jes/` · **US:** `/jes/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/y/yes/yes__/yes__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/y/yes/yes__/yes__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हाँ, हाज़िर हूँ, वाकई
- **Examples:**
  - ‘Is this your car?’ ‘Yes, it is.’
  - ‘Are you coming? Yes or no?’

<a id="w-252"></a>
### 252. [after](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/after_3) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɑːftə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈæftər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/aft/after/after__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/aft/after/after__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बाद में, पीछे
- **Examples:**
  - That was in 1996. Soon after, I heard that he'd died.
  - I could come next week, or the week after.
  - And they all lived happily ever after.

<a id="w-253"></a>
### 253. [since](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/since_3) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/sɪns/` · **US:** `/sɪns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sin/since/since__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sin/since/since__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तब  से, से, क्योंकि
- **Examples:**
  - She went for a run on Monday and has not been seen since.
  - She moved to Pakistan in 1997 and has been living in Lahore ever since.
  - He left home two weeks ago and we haven't heard from him since.
  - The health of the British people was better at the end of the Second World War than at any time before or since.

<a id="w-254"></a>
### 254. [long](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/long_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/lɒŋ/` · **US:** `/lɔːŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lon/long_/long__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lon/long_/long__us_8.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लम्बा, चिर
- **Examples:**
  - She had long dark hair.
  - He walked down the long corridor.
  - It was the world's longest bridge.
  - a long journey/walk/drive/flight

<a id="w-255"></a>
### 255. [provide](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/provide) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/prəˈvaɪd/` · **US:** `/prəˈvaɪd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pro/provi/provide__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pro/provi/provide__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** करना, देना, भरण पोषण करना
- **Examples:**
  - provide something Please provide the following information.
  - The exhibition provides an opportunity for local artists to show their work.
  - She did not provide any evidence to substantiate the claims.
  - The hospital has a commitment to provide the best possible medical care.

<a id="w-256"></a>
### 256. [service](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/service_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsɜːvɪs/` · **US:** `/ˈsɜːrvɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/ser/servi/service__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/ser/servi/service__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कार्य, कर्मचारी, सहायता
- **Examples:**
  - the ambulance/bus/postal service
  - The government aims to improve public services, especially education.
  - Essential services (= the supply of water, gas, electricity) will be maintained.
  - Councillors have called for better provision of services for young people.

<a id="w-257"></a>
### 257. [around](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/around_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈraʊnd/` · **US:** `/əˈraʊnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/aro/aroun/around__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/aro/aroun/around__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगभग, तक, उपलब्ध
- **Examples:**
  - He arrived around five o'clock.
  - The cost would be somewhere around £1 500.
  - There were only around 125 people present.
  - The correct figure is actually around double that amount.

<a id="w-258"></a>
### 258. [friend](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/friend_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/frend/` · **US:** `/frend/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fri/frien/friend__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fri/frien/friend__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** साथी, दोस्त, मिट्र
- **Examples:**
  - This is my friend Tom.
  - Is he a friend of yours?
  - Jane's a friend of the family.
  - She's an old friend (= I have known her a long time).

<a id="w-259"></a>
### 259. [important](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/important) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪmˈpɔːtnt/` · **US:** `/ɪmˈpɔːrtnt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/imp/impor/important__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/imp/impor/important__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शानदार, मुख्य, महत्वपूर्ण
- **Examples:**
  - an important issue/question/point/factor
  - We have an important decision to make.
  - Money played an important role in his life.
  - Listening is an important part of the job.

<a id="w-260"></a>
### 260. [father](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/father_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfɑːðə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈfɑːðər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fat/fathe/father__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fat/fathe/father__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पादरी, पूर्वज, जनक
- **Examples:**
  - My father died in 2017.
  - Ben's a wonderful father.
  - Our new boss is a father of three (= he has three children).
  - father to somebody You've been like a father to me.

<a id="w-261"></a>
### 261. [sit](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/sit) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/sɪt/` · **US:** `/sɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sit/sit__/sit__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sit/sit__/sit__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** रहने देना, देना, लगना
- **Examples:**
  - She sat and stared at the letter in front of her.
  - + adv./prep. May I sit here?
  - Don't just sit there—do something!
  - Just sit still!

<a id="w-262"></a>
### 262. [away](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/away_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈweɪ/` · **US:** `/əˈweɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/awa/away_/away__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/awa/away_/away__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगातार, दूर, बाहर
- **Examples:**
  - The beach is a mile away.
  - Christmas is still months away.
  - away from somebody/something The president was kept well away from the demonstrators.
  - The station is a few minutes' walk away from here.

<a id="w-263"></a>
### 263. [until](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/until) *(conjunction)*
- **UK:** `/ənˈtɪl/` · **US:** `/ənˈtɪl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/u/unt/until/until__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/u/unt/until/until__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तक/जब तक
- **Examples:**
  - Let's wait until the rain stops.
  - Until she spoke I hadn't realized she wasn't English.
  - You're not going out until you've finished this.
  - Until now I have always lived alone.

<a id="w-264"></a>
### 264. [power](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/power_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpaʊə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈpaʊər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pow/power/power__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pow/power/power__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बल, सत्ता, प्रभावशाली
- **Examples:**
  - power to do something He has the power to make things very unpleasant for us.
  - Religion is losing its power to shape our behaviour.
  - power over somebody/something The aim is to give people more power over their own lives.
  - The government wields enormous power over the economy.

<a id="w-265"></a>
### 265. [hour](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hour) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈaʊə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈaʊər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hou/hour_/hour__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hou/hour_/hour__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** घंटा
- **Examples:**
  - I spent an hour on the phone.
  - It will take about an hour to get there.
  - It usually takes me two hours to walk there.
  - The interview lasted half an hour.

<a id="w-266"></a>
### 266. [game](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/game_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡeɪm/` · **US:** `/ɡeɪm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gam/game_/game__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gam/game_/game__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कार्य, काम, चाल
- **Examples:**
  - The kids were playing a game with their balloons.
  - The children invented a new game.
  - a video/computer game
  - I play online games with my friends.

<a id="w-267"></a>
### 267. [often](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/often) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɒftən/` · **US:** `/ˈɔːftən/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/oft/often/often__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/oft/often/often__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अक्सर, प्रायः, बार
- **Examples:**
  - We often go there.
  - I've often wondered what happened to him.
  - How often do you go to the theatre?
  - I see her quite often.

<a id="w-268"></a>
### 268. [yet](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/yet_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/jet/` · **US:** `/jet/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/y/yet/yet__/yet__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/y/yet/yet__/yet__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अब तक, इस समय, फिर भी
- **Examples:**
  - (British English) I haven't received a letter from him yet.
  - (North American English) I didn't receive a letter from him yet.
  - not yet ‘Are you ready?’ ‘No, not yet.’
  - We don't yet know what really happened.

<a id="w-269"></a>
### 269. [line](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/line_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/laɪn/` · **US:** `/laɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lin/line_/line__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lin/line_/line__us_1.mp3)

  - a straight/solid/dotted/dashed line
  - a wavy/diagonal line
  - a vertical/horizontal line
  - parallel lines

### 270. [political](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/political) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/pəˈlɪtɪkl/` · **US:** `/pəˈlɪtɪkl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pol/polit/political__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pol/polit/political__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शासन विषयक, राजनैतिक, शासन  विषयक

  - a period of political stability
  - a political situation/crisis

<a id="w-271"></a>
### 271. [end](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/end_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/end/` · **US:** `/end/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/end/end__/end__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/end/end__/end__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समाप्ति, परिणाम, सीमा
- **Examples:**
  - It's the end of an era.
  - the end of the book
  - at the end of something at the end of the week
  - at the end There’ll be a chance to ask questions at the end.

<a id="w-272"></a>
### 272. [among](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/among) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/əˈmʌŋ/` · **US:** `/əˈmʌŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/amo/among/among__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/amo/among/among__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** में/मध्य में
- **Examples:**
  - a house among the trees
  - They strolled among the crowds.
  - I found the letter amongst his papers.
  - It's OK, you're among friends now.

<a id="w-273"></a>
### 273. [ever](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ever) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈevə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈevər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eve/ever_/ever__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eve/ever_/ever__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निरन्तर, नित्य, कदापि
- **Examples:**
  - Nothing ever happens here.
  - Don't you ever get tired?
  - If you're ever in Miami, come and see us.
  - ‘Have you ever been to Rome?’ ‘Yes, I have, actually. Not long ago.’

<a id="w-274"></a>
### 274. [stand](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/stand_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/stænd/` · **US:** `/stænd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sta/stand/stand__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sta/stand/stand__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कदम, दृष्टि, कटघरा
- **Examples:**
  - He has avoided taking a firm stand.
  - stand on something The judge takes a tough stand on corruption.
  - stand against something I admire their principled stand against the war.

<a id="w-275"></a>
### 275. [bad](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/bad_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/bæd/` · **US:** `/bæd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bad/bad__/bad__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bad/bad__/bad__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बुरा, गम्भीर, खराब
- **Examples:**
  - I have some bad news for you, I'm afraid.
  - bad weather/dreams/habits
  - I'm having a really bad day.
  - It was the worst experience of her life.

<a id="w-276"></a>
### 276. [lose](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/lose) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/luːz/` · **US:** `/luːz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/los/lose_/lose__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/los/lose_/lose__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पराजित होना, गँवा देना, हारना
- **Examples:**
  - I've lost my keys.
  - The tickets seem to have got lost.
  - She lost her husband in the crowd.

<a id="w-277"></a>
### 277. [however](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/however) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/haʊˈevə(r)/` · **US:** `/haʊˈevər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/how/howev/however__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/how/howev/however__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** फिर भी, तब भी, जैसे भी
- **Examples:**
  - He was feeling bad. He went to work, however, and tried to concentrate.
  - We thought the figures were correct. However, we have now discovered some errors.

<a id="w-278"></a>
### 278. [member](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/member) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈmembə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈmembər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mem/membe/member__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mem/membe/member__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** साथी, दल, हिस्सा
- **Examples:**
  - member of something a member of the family/community
  - He is an important member of our team.
  - a member of the human race
  - Members of the public are invited to come and view the work

<a id="w-279"></a>
### 279. [pay](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/pay_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/peɪ/` · **US:** `/peɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pay/pay__/pay__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pay/pay__/pay__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मूल्य, भुगतान, कमाई
- **Examples:**
  - Her job is hard work, but the pay is good.
  - workers on low pay
  - overtime/holiday pay
  - Her monthly take-home pay after taxes is $2 600.

<a id="w-280"></a>
### 280. [law](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/law) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/lɔː/` · **US:** `/lɔː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/law/law__/law__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/law/law__/law__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उपदेश, विधि, नियमावली
- **Examples:**
  - to pass a law (= officially make it part of the system of laws)
  - law against (doing) something Police don't have the resources to enforce the law against dumping waste.
  - (informal) There ought to be a law against it!
  - law on something The company's actions violated the law on equal employment.

<a id="w-281"></a>
### 281. [meet](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/meet_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/miːt/` · **US:** `/miːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mee/meet_/meet__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mee/meet_/meet__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** होना, मिलना, स्पर्श करना
- **Examples:**
  - Maybe we'll meet again some time.
  - meet somebody Did you meet anyone in town?
  - I've never met anyone like her.
  - A year or so later I happened to meet him again.

<a id="w-282"></a>
### 282. [car](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/car) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kɑː(r)/` · **US:** `/kɑːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/car/car__/car__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/car/car__/car__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-283"></a>
### 283. [city](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/city) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsɪti/` · **US:** `/ˈsɪti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cit/city_/city__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cit/city_/city__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-284"></a>
### 284. [almost](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/almost) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɔːlməʊst/` · **US:** `/ˈɔːlməʊst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/alm/almos/almost__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/alm/almos/almost__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगभग, प्रायः, करीब करीब
- **Examples:**
  - I like almost all of them.
  - They'll eat almost anything.
  - Almost no one (= hardly anyone) believed him.
  - The castle is almost entirely surrounded by water.

<a id="w-285"></a>
### 285. [include](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/include) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈkluːd/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈkluːd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inc/inclu/include__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inc/inclu/include__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दर्ज़ करना, शामिल होना, मिलाना
- **Examples:**
  - include something The tour included a visit to the Science Museum.
  - Does the price include tax?
  - The report includes a number of recommendations.
  - include doing something Your duties will include greeting visitors and directing them to the appropriate department.

<a id="w-286"></a>
### 286. [continue](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/continue) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈtɪnjuː/` · **US:** `/kənˈtɪnjuː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontinue__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontinue__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** होना, शुरू  करना, रखना
- **Examples:**
  - If the current trend continues, that number will increase 165 per cent by 2050.
  - Police have not yet made any arrests, but investigations continue.
  - + adv./prep. The exhibition continues until 25 July.
  - Fighting continued for four years.

### 289. [community](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/community) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kəˈmjuːnəti/` · **US:** `/kəˈmjuːnəti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/commu/community__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/commu/community__us_2.mp3)

### 290. [much](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/much_2) *(adverb)*

### 291. [name](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/name_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/neɪm/` · **US:** `/neɪm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nam/name_/name__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nam/name_/name__us_1.mp3)

  - Please write your full name and address below.

<a id="w-292"></a>
### 292. [five](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/five) *(number)*

<a id="w-295"></a>
### 295. [least](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/least_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/liːst/` · **US:** `/liːst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lea/least/least__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lea/least/least__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कम से कम, कम, सब से  छोटा
- **Examples:**
  - He always turns up just when you least expect him.
  - She chose the least expensive of the hotels.
  - I never hid the truth, least of all from you.
  - We had to settle for the least worst option (= the best of several options that were all bad).

<a id="w-296"></a>
### 296. [president](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/president) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈprezɪdənt/` · **US:** `/ˈprezɪdənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/presi/president__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/presi/president__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रधान, अध्यक्ष, कार्यकारी व्यवस्थापक
- **Examples:**
  - Several presidents attended the funeral.
  - the President of the United States
  - President Biden is due to visit the country next month.
  - Do you have any comment, Mr President?

<a id="w-297"></a>
### 297. [learn](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/learn) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/lɜːn/` · **US:** `/lɜːrn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lea/learn/learn__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lea/learn/learn__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विचार करना, सूचना पाना, सीखना
- **Examples:**
  - learn something to learn a language/skill/trade
  - He had the opportunity to learn English in Australia.
  - learn (something) from somebody/something I learned a lot from my father.
  - Everyone in the class had the opportunity to learn from each other.

### 298. [real](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/real_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/rɪəl/` · **US:** `/ˈriːəl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rea/real_/real__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rea/real_/real__us_2_rr.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - a real danger/risk/threat/concern
  - All the characters are based on real people.
  - It wasn't a ghost; it was a real person.
  - pictures of animals, both real and mythological

<a id="w-299"></a>
### 299. [change](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/change_2) *(noun)*

- **Meaning:** परिवर्तन, छोटा सिक्का, बद्लाव
- **Examples:**
  - change in something We're hoping for a change in the weather.
  - There was no change in the patient's condition overnight.
  - I need to make some major changes in my life.
  - a change in policy/law

  - We played against a team from the neighbouring school.

<a id="w-301"></a>
### 301. [minute](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/minute2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/maɪˈnjuːt/` · **US:** `/maɪˈnuːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/min/minut/minute__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/min/minut/minute__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मिनटअ, तैयार करना, क्षण
- **Examples:**
  - minute amounts of chemicals in the water
  - The kitchen on the boat is minute.

<a id="w-302"></a>
### 302. [best](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/best_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/best/` · **US:** `/best/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bes/best_/best__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bes/best_/best__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सर्वोत्तम, बेहतरीन
- **Examples:**
  - That's the best movie I've ever seen!
  - He wrote his best songs before he was 25.
  - She was one of the best tennis players of her generation.
  - Is that your best suit?

<a id="w-303"></a>
### 303. [several](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/several_1) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsevrəl/` · **US:** `/ˈsevrəl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sev/sever/several__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sev/sever/several__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रकार, भिन्न, बहुत
- **Examples:**
  - Several letters arrived this morning.
  - He's written several books about India.
  - Several more people than usual came to the meeting.
  - If you're looking for a photo of Alice you'll find several in here.

<a id="w-304"></a>
### 304. [idea](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/idea) *(noun)*

- **Meaning:** तर्क, जानकारी, धारणा
- **Examples:**

  - That's a great idea!
  - I’ve had a brilliant idea!

<a id="w-308"></a>
### 308. [nothing](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/nothing) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnʌθɪŋ/` · **US:** `/ˈnʌθɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/not/nothi/nothing__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/not/nothi/nothing__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कुछ नहीं
- **Examples:**

  - There's nothing you can do to help.

<a id="w-309"></a>
### 309. [ago](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ago) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈɡəʊ/` · **US:** `/əˈɡəʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ago/ago__/ago__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ago/ago__/ago__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पहले, पहले{बीता हुआ}
- **Examples:**
  - two weeks/months/years ago
  - The letter came a few days ago.
  - She was here just a minute ago.
  - a short/long time ago

<a id="w-310"></a>
### 310. [right](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/right_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/raɪt/` · **US:** `/raɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rig/right/right__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rig/right/right__us_1.mp3)

### 311. [lead](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/lead1_2#lead_hm1_sng_21) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/liːd/` · **US:** `/liːd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lea/lead_/lead__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lea/lead_/lead__us_1.mp3)

### 312. [social](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/social_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsəʊʃl/` · **US:** `/ˈsəʊʃl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/soc/socia/social__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/soc/socia/social__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-313"></a>
### 313. [understand](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/understand) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˌʌndəˈstænd/` · **US:** `/ˌʌndərˈstænd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/u/und/under/understand__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/u/und/under/understand__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्वीकार करना, समझना, जानना

  - understand (something) Can you understand French?
  - Do you understand the instructions?
  - She didn't understand the form she was signing.
  - His accent made him difficult to understand.

<a id="w-314"></a>
### 314. [whether](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/whether) *(conjunction)*
- **UK:** `/ˈweðə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈweðər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/whe/wheth/whether__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/whe/wheth/whether__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** चाहें, कि
- **Examples:**

  - It remains to be seen whether or not this idea can be put into practice.
  - I asked him whether he had done it all himself or whether someone had helped him.
  - I'll see whether she's at home (= or not at home).

### 315. [back](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/back_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/bæk/` · **US:** `/bæk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bac/back_/back__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bac/back_/back__us_1.mp3)

### 316. [watch](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/watch_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/wɒtʃ/` · **US:** `/wɑːtʃ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wat/watch/watch__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wat/watch/watch__us_6.mp3)

<a id="w-317"></a>
### 317. [together](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/together_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/təˈɡeðə(r)/` · **US:** `/təˈɡeðər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tog/toget/together__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tog/toget/together__us_1.mp3)

  - Do you want to get together (= meet) again next week?

<a id="w-318"></a>
### 318. [follow](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/follow) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfɒləʊ/` · **US:** `/ˈfɑːləʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fol/follo/follow__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fol/follo/follow__us_1.mp3)

  - They were followed down the street by a bunch of photographers.
  - Wherever she led, they followed.

<a id="w-319"></a>
### 319. [around](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/around_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈraʊnd/` · **US:** `/əˈraʊnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/aro/aroun/around__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/aro/aroun/around__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगभग, तक, उपलब्ध
- **Examples:**
  - He arrived around five o'clock.
  - The cost would be somewhere around £1 500.
  - There were only around 125 people present.
  - The correct figure is actually around double that amount.

- **UK:** `/ˈpeərənt/` · **US:** `/ˈperənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/par/paren/parent__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/par/paren/parent__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/feɪs/` · **US:** `/feɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fac/face_/face__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fac/face_/face__us_1.mp3)

  - a pretty/pale/round face

  - to hide/cover your face

<a id="w-324"></a>
### 324. [anything](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/anything) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈeniθɪŋ/` · **US:** `/ˈeniθɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/any/anyth/anything__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/any/anyth/anything__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कुछ, कुछ भी
- **Examples:**
  - Would you like anything else?
  - There's never anything worth watching on TV.
  - If you remember anything at all, please let us know.

<a id="w-325"></a>
### 325. [create](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/create) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kriˈeɪt/` · **US:** `/kriˈeɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cre/creat/create__gb_5.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cre/creat/create__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तैयार  करना, तैयार करना, बनाना
- **Examples:**
  - Scientists disagree about how the universe was created.
  - The main purpose of industry is to create wealth.
  - The government plans to create more jobs for young people.
  - Create a new directory and put all your files into it.

<a id="w-326"></a>
### 326. [public](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/public_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpʌblɪk/` · **US:** `/ˈpʌblɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pub/publi/public__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pub/publi/public__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सार्वजनिक
- **Examples:**
  - The campaign is designed to increase public awareness of the issues.
  - Levels of waste from the factory may be a danger to public health.
  - Public safety must come first.
  - Why would the closure of hospitals be in the public interest (= useful to ordinary people)?

<a id="w-327"></a>
### 327. [already](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/already) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ɔːlˈredi/` · **US:** `/ɔːlˈredi/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/alr/alrea/already__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/alr/alrea/already__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पहले से, पहले ही, पहले से ही
- **Examples:**
  - ‘Lunch?’ ‘No thanks, I've already eaten.’
  - We got there early but Mike had already left.
  - By 2015, filming of the sequel was already under way.
  - Much of what he said I knew already.

<a id="w-328"></a>
### 328. [speak](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/speak_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/spiːk/` · **US:** `/spiːk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/spe/speak/speak__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/spe/speak/speak__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बताना, बजना, प्रकट करना
- **Examples:**
  - speak to somebody The President refused to speak to reporters.
  - speak about something We never spoke about her illness.
  - The confidential setting allowed us to speak freely about our concerns.
  - speak to somebody about something/somebody I've spoken to the manager about it.

- **UK:** `/riːd/` · **US:** `/riːd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rea/read_/read__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rea/read_/read__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बताना, दिखाना, समझना

  - Can you read music?

<a id="w-331"></a>
### 331. [level](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/level_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈlevl/` · **US:** `/ˈlevl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lev/level/level__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lev/level/level__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** परत, सपाट, समतल
- **Examples:**
  - Pitch the tent on level ground.

<a id="w-332"></a>
### 332. [allow](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/allow) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈlaʊ/` · **US:** `/əˈlaʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/all/allow/allow__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/all/allow/allow__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्वीकार करना, मिलना, देना
- **Examples:**
  - allow somebody/something to do something His parents won't allow him to stay out late.
  - He allowed his mind to wander.
  - The law allowed companies to dismiss workers without any reason.
  - be allowed to do something He is not allowed to stay out late.

<a id="w-333"></a>
### 333. [add](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/add_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/æd/` · **US:** `/æd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/add/add__/add__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/add/add__/add__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जुड़ना, देना, मिलाना
- **Examples:**
  - add something Next add the flour.
  - The juice contains no added sugar.
  - As an added bonus, the book includes many black-and-white photographs.
  - Melt the butter, then add the onion.

### 334. [office](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/office) *(noun)*

- **Meaning:** पद, विभाग, कार्यालय
- **Examples:**

  - at the office I've had a tough day at the office.

<a id="w-335"></a>
### 335. [spend](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/spend_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/spend/` · **US:** `/spend/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/spe/spend/spend__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/spe/spend/spend__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगाना, बिताना, व्यय करना

- **UK:** `/ˈpɜːsn/` · **US:** `/ˈpɜːrsn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/perso/person__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/perso/person__us_2.mp3)

- **Meaning:** स्थिर, आश्वस्त, अचूक
- **Examples:**
  - ‘Is that John over there?’ ‘I'm not sure.’
  - You don't sound very sure.
  - sure (that)… I'm pretty sure (that) he'll agree.

<a id="w-341"></a>
### 341. [such](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/such) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/sʌtʃ/` · **US:** `/sʌtʃ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/suc/such_/such__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/suc/such_/such__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इस तरह का, वैसा ही, इस
- **Examples:**
  - This issue was of such importance that we could not afford to ignore it.
  - such a/an… Why are you in such a hurry?
  - It's such a beautiful day!
  - such is something that… (formal) Such is the elegance of this typeface that it is still a favourite of designers.

<a id="w-345"></a>
### 345. [within](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/within_1) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/wɪˈðɪn/` · **US:** `/wɪˈðɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wit/withi/within__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wit/withi/within__us_1_rr.mp3)

<a id="w-346"></a>
### 346. [grow](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/grow) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɡrəʊ/` · **US:** `/ɡrəʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gro/grow_/grow__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gro/grow_/grow__us_1.mp3)

### 374. [second](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/second1_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsekənd/` · **US:** `/ˈsekənd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sec/secon/second__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sec/secon/second__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्थानान्तरित करना, सहारा देना, नियुक्त करना
- **Examples:**
  - She came second in the marathon.
  - One of the smaller parties came a close second (= nearly won).
  - The women's team came in fourth and the men finished second.
  - I agreed to speak second.

<a id="w-375"></a>
### 375. [boy](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/boy_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/bɔɪ/` · **US:** `/bɔɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/boy/boy__/boy__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/boy/boy__/boy__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दोस्त, नौकर, बालक
- **Examples:**
  - a little/small/young boy

  - Now she's a teenager, she's starting to be interested in boys.

<a id="w-376"></a>
### 376. [maybe](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/maybe_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈmeɪbi/` · **US:** `/ˈmeɪbi/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/may/maybe/maybe__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/may/maybe/maybe__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सम्भवतः, शायद
- **Examples:**

  - Is she right? Maybe, maybe not.
  - Maybe it will do me some good to go for a run.

<a id="w-381"></a>
### 381. [policy](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/policy) *(noun)*

  - policy of something We have tried to pursue a policy of neutrality.

### 382. [everything](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/everything) *(pronoun)*

- **UK:** `/ˈprəʊses/` · **US:** `/ˈprɑːses/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pro/proce/process__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pro/proce/process__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-386"></a>
### 386. [including](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/including) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈkluːdɪŋ/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈkluːdɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inc/inclu/including__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inc/inclu/including__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सम्मिलित करते हुए
- **Examples:**
  - I've got three days' holiday including New Year's Day.
  - Six people were killed in the riot, including a policeman.

### 387. [consider](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consider) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsɪdə(r)/` · **US:** `/kənˈsɪdər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsider__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsider__us_1.mp3)

  - Let us consider the facts.

### 388. [appear](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/appear) *(verb)*

- **Meaning:** होना, प्रकट होना, दिखाई देना
- **Examples:**
  - Three days later a rash appeared.
  - Symptoms usually start appearing within two to three days.
  - + adv./prep. A cat suddenly appeared out of nowhere.
  - Smoke appeared on the horizon.

### 389. [actually](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/actually) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈæktʃuəli/` · **US:** `/ˈæktʃuəli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/act/actua/actually__gb_5.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/act/actua/actually__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वास्तव में, वस्तु, सचमुच
- **Examples:**
  - What did she actually say?
  - It's not actually raining now.
  - The book was never actually published.
  - I'm glad we were actually able to get the message across.

### 390. [buy](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/buy_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/baɪ/` · **US:** `/baɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/buy/buy__/buy__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/buy/buy__/buy__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्वीकार करना, प्राप्त करना, ख़रीदना
- **Examples:**
  - buy (something) Where did you buy that dress?
  - She had no money to buy a ticket.
  - They can't afford to buy school books.
  - If you're thinking of getting a new car, now is a good time to buy.

<a id="w-391"></a>
### 391. [probably](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/probably) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈprɒbəbli/` · **US:** `/ˈprɑːbəbli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pro/proba/probably__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pro/proba/probably__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सम्भवतः, शायद, संभवतः
- **Examples:**
  - You're probably right.
  - I think that's probably true.
  - It'll probably be OK.
  - It was the best known and probably the most popular of her songs.

- **UK:** `/weɪt/` · **US:** `/weɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wai/wait_/wait__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wai/wait_/wait__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इन्तजार, काम, इंतज़ार
- **Examples:**
  - The wait seemed interminable.

  - Pour the sauce over the pasta and serve immediately.

### 396. [die](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/die_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/daɪ/` · **US:** `/daɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/die/die__/die__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/die/die__/die__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** होना, बंद होना, मरना

- **UK:** `/send/` · **US:** `/send/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sen/send_/send__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sen/send_/send__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निकलना, उकस्ना, प्रेषित करना

  - Let me just send this email.

  - (North American English) to send something by mail

<a id="w-398"></a>
### 398. [expect](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/expect) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪkˈspekt/` · **US:** `/ɪkˈspekt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exp/expec/expect__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exp/expec/expect__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आशा करना, आसरा देखना, रख लेना
- **Examples:**
  - expect something The company is expecting record sales this year.
  - Change often happens when you least expect it.
  - expect something from somebody/something Don't expect sympathy from me!
  - expect something of somebody/something That's not the sort of behaviour I expect of you!

- **UK:** `/həʊm/` · **US:** `/həʊm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hom/home_/home__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hom/home_/home__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** घरेलू
- **Examples:**

  - a person’s home address/country
  - We offer customers a free home delivery service.

<a id="w-400"></a>
### 400. [sense](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/sense_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/sens/` · **US:** `/sens/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sen/sense/sense__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sen/sense/sense__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दिशा, होश, ज्ञान
- **Examples:**
  - One of the most important things in a partner is a sense of humour (= the ability to find things funny or make people laugh).
  - He has a very good sense of direction (= finds the way to a place easily).
  - (figurative) She has lost all sense of direction in her life (= the idea of what she should do in her life).
  - Always try to keep a sense of proportion (= of the relative importance of different things).
