## Words 401 – 500

<a id="w-401"></a>
### 401. [build](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/build_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/bɪld/` · **US:** `/bɪld/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bui/build/build__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bui/build/build__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** करना, बढ़ाना, बनाना
- **Examples:**
  - build (something) They have permission to build 200 new homes.
  - to build a plant/facility/structure
  - We build computer systems for large companies.
  - Robins build nests almost anywhere.

<a id="w-402"></a>
### 402. [stay](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/stay_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/steɪ/` · **US:** `/steɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sta/stay_/stay__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sta/stay_/stay__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ठहराव, प्रवास, तान  रस्सी
- **Examples:**
  - I enjoyed my stay in Prague.
  - an overnight stay
  - In recent years the average hospital stay for elderly patients has decreased.

<a id="w-403"></a>
### 403. [fall](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/fall_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/fɔːl/` · **US:** `/fɔːl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fal/fall_/fall__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fal/fall_/fall__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** गिरावट, पतन, पराजय
- **Examples:**
  - I had a bad fall and broke my arm.
  - fall from something She was killed in a fall from a horse.

<a id="w-404"></a>
### 404. [oh](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/oh_1) *(exclamation)*
- **UK:** `/əʊ/` · **US:** `/əʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/owe/owe__/owe__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/oh_/oh__u/oh__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आह, ओह
- **Examples:**
  - ‘I saw Ben yesterday.’ ‘Oh yes, how is he?’
  - Oh dear! What's happened now?
  - You can’t come tonight? Oh well, see you next week then.
  - ‘Emma has a new job.’ ‘Oh, has she?’

<a id="w-405"></a>
### 405. [nation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/nation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈneɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˈneɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nat/natio/nation__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nat/natio/nation__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जाति, राज्य, राष्ट्र
- **Examples:**
  - European/Arab/Asian nations
  - The West African nation of Togo has presidential elections this week.
  - Developing nations need affordable access to drugs and medication.
  - Fewer and fewer people in industrialized nations actually know where their food comes from.

<a id="w-406"></a>
### 406. [plan](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/plan_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/plæn/` · **US:** `/plæn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pla/plan_/plan__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pla/plan_/plan__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नक़्शा, व्यवस्था, मापचित्र
- **Examples:**
  - plan for something Do you have any plans for the summer?
  - plan to do something There are no plans to build new offices.
  - Your best plan (= the best thing to do) would be to go by car.
  - There's been a change of plan.

<a id="w-407"></a>
### 407. [cut](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/cut_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kʌt/` · **US:** `/kʌt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cut/cut__/cut__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cut/cut__/cut__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** घाव, हिस्सा, चीरा
- **Examples:**
  - cuts and bruises on the face
  - Blood poured from the deep cut on his arm.

<a id="w-408"></a>
### 408. [college](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/college) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒlɪdʒ/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːlɪdʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/col/colle/college__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/col/colle/college__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संघ, कालेज, केंद्र
- **Examples:**
  - a secretarial college
  - the Royal College of Art
  - a college course/student
  - She wanted to go to college to become a nurse.

<a id="w-409"></a>
### 409. [interest](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/interest_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪntrest/` · **US:** `/ˈɪntrest/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inter/interest__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inter/interest__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** महत्व, दल, दिलचस्पी
- **Examples:**
  - Her research has generated interest around the world.
  - The case has attracted the interest of the public and media.
  - interest in something Do your parents take an interest in your friends?
  - By that time I had lost (all) interest in the idea.

<a id="w-410"></a>
### 410. [death](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/death) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/deθ/` · **US:** `/deθ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dea/death/death__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dea/death/death__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मृत्यु, मृत्यु/मरण, आकस्मिक  मृत्यु
- **Examples:**
  - the anniversary of his wife’s death
  - his sudden/untimely/premature death
  - the tragic death of a child
  - The jury returned a verdict of accidental death.

<a id="w-411"></a>
### 411. [course](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/course_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kɔːs/` · **US:** `/kɔːrs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cou/cours/course__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cou/cours/course__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** क्रम, श्रेणी, प्रवाह
- **Examples:**
  - a French/chemistry course
  - course in something He took a course in Russian language and literature.
  - I want to do a course in art and design.
  - The department offers short courses in drama, fiction, and poetry.

<a id="w-412"></a>
### 412. [someone](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/someone) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsʌmwʌn/` · **US:** `/ˈsʌmwʌn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/som/someo/someone__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/som/someo/someone__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कोई, बड़ा आदमी, कोई{व्यक्ति}
- **Examples:**
  - There's someone at the door.
  - Someone's left their bag behind.
  - It's time for someone new (= a new person) to take over.
  - It couldn't have been me—it must have been someone else (= a different person).

<a id="w-413"></a>
### 413. [experience](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/experience_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪkˈspɪəriəns/` · **US:** `/ɪkˈspɪriəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exp/exper/experience__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exp/exper/experience__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अनुभव, अनुभूति, तज़ुर्बा
- **Examples:**
  - My lack of practical experience was a disadvantage.
  - experience of something Do you have any previous experience of this type of work?
  - This new approach draws on years of experience of teaching children to read.
  - experience in doing something a doctor with experience in dealing with patients suffering from stress

<a id="w-414"></a>
### 414. [behind](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/behind_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/bɪˈhaɪnd/` · **US:** `/bɪˈhaɪnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/beh/behin/behind__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/beh/behin/behind__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पीछे से
- **Examples:**
  - She rode off down the road with the dog running behind.
  - The others are a long way behind.
  - He was shot from behind as he ran away.
  - I had fallen so far behind that it seemed pointless trying to catch up.

<a id="w-415"></a>
### 415. [reach](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/reach_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/riːtʃ/` · **US:** `/riːtʃ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rea/reach/reach__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rea/reach/reach__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** फैलाव, समतल भाग
- **Examples:**
  - beyond somebody's reach The shot was well beyond the reach of the goalkeeper.
  - out of somebody's reach Keep all medicines out of reach of children.
  - within somebody's reach He lashed out angrily, hitting anyone within his reach.
  - It's a book I like to keep within arm's reach.

<a id="w-416"></a>
### 416. [local](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/local_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈləʊkl/` · **US:** `/ˈləʊkl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/loc/local/local__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/loc/local/local__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सीमित, स्थानीय
- **Examples:**
  - local people/residents/businesses
  - members of the local community
  - the local police
  - local elections

<a id="w-417"></a>
### 417. [kill](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/kill_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kɪl/` · **US:** `/kɪl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/k/kil/kill_/kill__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/k/kil/kill_/kill__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** परेशान करना, शांत करना, समाप्त करना
- **Examples:**
  - kill (somebody/something) Cancer kills thousands of people every year.
  - Tiredness while driving can kill.
  - Dozens of civilians were killed or injured in the attack.
  - She was nearly killed by a car bomb.

<a id="w-418"></a>
### 418. [six](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/six) *(number)*
- **UK:** `/sɪks/` · **US:** `/sɪks/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/six/six__/six__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/six/six__/six__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** छः
- **Examples:**
  - There are six cookies left.
  - six of Sweden’s top financial experts
  - Ten people were invited but only six turned up.
  - Can you lend me six dollars?

<a id="w-419"></a>
### 419. [remain](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/remain) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈmeɪn/` · **US:** `/rɪˈmeɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rem/remai/remain__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rem/remai/remain__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** रहना, बाकी रहना, भग्नावशेष
- **Examples:**
  - remain + adj. to remain silent
  - to remain standing/seated/motionless
  - to remain calm/confident
  - Train fares are likely to remain unchanged.

<a id="w-420"></a>
### 420. [effect](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/effect_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈfekt/` · **US:** `/ɪˈfekt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eff/effec/effect__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eff/effec/effect__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रभाव, असर, परिणाम
- **Examples:**
  - the beneficial effects of exercise
  - What are the long-term effects of this treatment?
  - The results show a statistically significant effect.
  - effect on somebody/something Her tears had no effect on him.

<a id="w-421"></a>
### 421. [use](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/use_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/juːs/` · **US:** `/juːs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/u/use/use__/use__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/u/use/use__/use__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लाभ/प्रयोजन, फ़ायदा, प्रयोजन
- **Examples:**
  - A ban was imposed on the use of chemical weapons.
  - You can't justify the use of military force.
  - I'm not sure that this is the most valuable use of my time.
  - for… use These videos are intended for classroom use.

<a id="w-422"></a>
### 422. [yeah](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/yeah) *(exclamation)*
- **UK:** `/jeə/` · **US:** `/jeə/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/y/yea/yeah_/yeah__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/y/yea/yeah_/yeah__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हाँ

<a id="w-423"></a>
### 423. [suggest](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/suggest) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/səˈdʒest/` · **US:** `/səɡˈdʒest/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sug/sugge/suggest__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sug/sugge/suggest__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बताना, प्रस्ताव रखना, संकेत करना
- **Examples:**
  - suggest something I'd like to suggest a different explanation for the company's decline.
  - suggest something to somebody The designer is known for suggesting innovative ideas to her clients.
  - suggest itself He thought long and hard, but no answer suggested itself.
  - suggest itself to somebody A solution immediately suggested itself to me (= I immediately thought of a solution).

<a id="w-424"></a>
### 424. [class](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/class_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/klɑːs/` · **US:** `/klæs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cla/class/class__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cla/class/class__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दर्जा, उतम, वर्ग
- **Examples:**
  - in the/your class We were in the same class at school.
  - She is the youngest in her class.
  - He came top of the class.
  - The whole class was/were told to stay behind after school.

<a id="w-425"></a>
### 425. [control](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/control_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈtrəʊl/` · **US:** `/kənˈtrəʊl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontrol__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcont/xcontrol__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शासन, निपुणता, नियंत्रण
- **Examples:**
  - control of somebody/something The party expects to gain control of the council in the next election.
  - Militants have taken control of the town.
  - The Democrats have lost control of Congress.
  - A military junta seized control of the country.

<a id="w-426"></a>
### 426. [raise](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/raise_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/reɪz/` · **US:** `/reɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rai/raise/raise__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rai/raise/raise__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** करना, निकालना, उत्तेजित करना
- **Examples:**
  - She raised the gun and fired.
  - He raised a hand in greeting.
  - She raised her eyes from her work.
  - raise something up He slowly raised his head up.

<a id="w-427"></a>
### 427. [care](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/care_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/keə(r)/` · **US:** `/ker/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/car/care_/care__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/car/care_/care__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इच्छा, ध्यान, संरक्षण
- **Examples:**
  - Some people were badly injured and needed medical care.
  - We aim to continually improve the quality of patient care.
  - How much do men share housework and the care of the children?
  - care for somebody/something to provide care for the elderly

<a id="w-428"></a>
### 428. [perhaps](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/perhaps) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/præps/` · **US:** `/pərˈhæps/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/perha/perhaps__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/perha/perhaps__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हो  सकता है, शायद, संभवतः
- **Examples:**
  - ‘Are you going to come?’ ‘Perhaps. I'll see how I feel.’
  - Perhaps he's forgotten.

<a id="w-429"></a>
### 429. [little](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/little_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈlɪtl/` · **US:** `/ˈlɪtl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lit/littl/little__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lit/littl/little__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** छोटा, महत्वहीन, थोडा
- **Examples:**
  - a little house
  - a little group of tourists
  - a little old lady
  - We passed through several nice little towns.

<a id="w-430"></a>
### 430. [late](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/late_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/leɪt/` · **US:** `/leɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lat/late_/late__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lat/late_/late__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** देर रात की, दिवंगत, देर से
- **Examples:**
  - I'm sorry I'm late.
  - late for something She's late for work every day.
  - My flight was an hour late.
  - We apologize for the late arrival of this train.

<a id="w-431"></a>
### 431. [hard](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hard_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/hɑːd/` · **US:** `/hɑːrd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/har/hard_/hard__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/har/hard_/hard__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अटल, कटु, नृशंस
- **Examples:**
  - a hard choice/decision/question
  - hard to do something It is hard to believe that she's only nine.
  - It's hard to see how they can lose.
  - It's hard to imagine a more cynical political strategy.

<a id="w-432"></a>
### 432. [field](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/field_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/fiːld/` · **US:** `/fiːld/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fie/field/field__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fie/field/field__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कार्य क्षेत्र, आधार, क्षेत्र
- **Examples:**
  - We had to walk across a ploughed field.
  - in a field We camped in a field near the village.
  - People were working in the fields.
  - field of something We saw golden fields of wheat.

<a id="w-433"></a>
### 433. [else](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/else) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/els/` · **US:** `/els/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/els/else_/else__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/els/else_/else__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** और कौन
- **Examples:**
  - What else did he say?
  - I don't want anything else, thanks.
  - I'm taking a few clothes and some books, not much else.

<a id="w-434"></a>
### 434. [pass](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/pass_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pɑːs/` · **US:** `/pæs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pas/pass_/pass__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pas/pass_/pass__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संकटापन्न अवस्था, दर्रा, सफलता
- **Examples:**
  - a boarding pass (= for a plane)
  - There is no admittance without a security pass.
  - We had backstage passes to the show.

<a id="w-435"></a>
### 435. [former](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/former_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfɔːmə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈfɔːrmər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/for/forme/former__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/for/forme/former__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पुराना, भूतपूर्व, पूर्व
- **Examples:**
  - This fine ruin was, in former times, a royal castle.
  - This beautiful old building has been restored to its former glory.
  - her former life as a Russian ballerina

<a id="w-436"></a>
### 436. [sell](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/sell_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/sel/` · **US:** `/sel/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sel/sell_/sell__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sel/sell_/sell__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** व्यापार करना, थोपना, सौदा करना
- **Examples:**
  - sell (something) I recently sold my bike.
  - His father went bankrupt and the family had to sell their home.
  - The board agreed to sell the company.
  - The shares were sold in the open market.

<a id="w-437"></a>
### 437. [major](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/major_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈmeɪdʒə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈmeɪdʒər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/maj/major/major__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/maj/major/major__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रमुख, मुख्य, महत्वपूर्ण
- **Examples:**
  - a major road
  - Some major international companies refused to do business with them.
  - He played a major role in setting up the system.
  - major sporting events

<a id="w-438"></a>
### 438. [sometimes](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/sometimes) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsʌmtaɪmz/` · **US:** `/ˈsʌmtaɪmz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/som/somet/sometimes__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/som/somet/sometimes__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कभी कभी, कभी भी, थोड़े समय से
- **Examples:**
  - Sometimes I go by car.
  - He sometimes writes to me.
  - I like to be on my own sometimes.
  - The skiing season runs from October to late April, and sometimes even into May.

<a id="w-439"></a>
### 439. [require](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/require) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈkwaɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/rɪˈkwaɪər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/req/requi/require__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/req/requi/require__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** चाहना, आदेश देना, इच्छा करना
- **Examples:**
  - require something These pets require a lot of care and attention.
  - Deciphering the code requires an expert.
  - This condition requires urgent treatment.
  - Do you require anything else? (= in a shop/store, for example)

<a id="w-440"></a>
### 440. [along](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/along_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈlɒŋ/` · **US:** `/əˈlɔːŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/alo/along/along__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/alo/along/along__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आगे बढना, साथ, आगे
- **Examples:**
  - I was just walking along singing to myself.
  - He pointed out various landmarks as we drove along.

<a id="w-441"></a>
### 441. [development](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/development) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈveləpmənt/` · **US:** `/dɪˈveləpmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dev/devel/development__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dev/devel/development__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सुधार, विस्तार, आविष्कार
- **Examples:**
  - a baby’s development in the womb
  - This is a perfectly normal stage of development.
  - the development of basic skills such as literacy and numeracy
  - The company can offer a number of opportunities for career development.

### 443. [report](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/report_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈpɔːt/` · **US:** `/rɪˈpɔːrt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rep/repor/report__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rep/repor/report__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विवरण, रिपोर्ट, जानकारी

  - media/press/newspaper reports

<a id="w-444"></a>
### 444. [role](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/role) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rəʊl/` · **US:** `/rəʊl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rol/role_/role__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rol/role_/role__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कर्तव्य, भूमिका

  - She has landed the lead role (= the most important one) in a new play.
  - a leading/starring role

<a id="w-445"></a>
### 445. [better](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/better_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈbetə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈbetər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bet/bette/better__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bet/bette/better__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बेहतर
- **Examples:**
  - We're hoping for better weather tomorrow.
  - Her work is getting better and better.
  - He is in a much better mood than usual.
  - The meal couldn't have been better.

<a id="w-446"></a>
### 446. [economic](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/economic) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˌekəˈnɒmɪk/` · **US:** `/ˌekəˈnɑːmɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eco/econo/economic__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eco/econo/economic__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अर्थशास्त्रीय, आर्थिक
- **Examples:**
  - They discussed social, economic and political issues.
  - economic growth/development
  - the worst economic crisis since the war
  - There will be no quick economic recovery.

<a id="w-447"></a>
### 447. [effort](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/effort) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈefət/` · **US:** `/ˈefərt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eff/effor/effort__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eff/effor/effort__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रयास, प्रयत्न, सफलता
- **Examples:**
  - The project was a team effort.
  - a joint/collaborative effort
  - a determined/conscious/special effort
  - I didn't really feel like going out, but I am glad I made the effort.

<a id="w-448"></a>
### 448. [decide](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/decide) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈsaɪd/` · **US:** `/dɪˈsaɪd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dec/decid/decide__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dec/decid/decide__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निश्चित करना, तय करना, फ़ैसला सुनाना
- **Examples:**
  - It's up to you to decide.
  - You will have to decide soon.
  - I can't tell you what to do—you'll have to decide for yourself.
  - decide to do something We've decided not to go away after all.

<a id="w-449"></a>
### 449. [rate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/rate_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/reɪt/` · **US:** `/reɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rat/rate_/rate__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rat/rate_/rate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दर्जा, कोटि, पौरकर उपशुल्क
- **Examples:**
  - rate (of something) Figures published today show another fall in the rate of inflation.
  - at a/the rate of something Most people walk at an average rate of five kilometres an hour.
  - at a… rate The number of reported crimes is increasing at an alarming rate.
  - rate (that)… At the rate you work, you'll never finish!

<a id="w-450"></a>
### 450. [strong](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/strong) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/strɒŋ/` · **US:** `/strɔːŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/str/stron/strong__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/str/stron/strong__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** घनिष्ठ, भारी, अटल
- **Examples:**
  - He's strong enough to lift a car!
  - strong muscles
  - She wasn't a strong swimmer (= she could not swim well).

<a id="w-451"></a>
### 451. [possible](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/possible_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpɒsəbl/` · **US:** `/ˈpɑːsəbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pos/possi/possible__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pos/possi/possible__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संभव, सत्याभासी
- **Examples:**

  - it is possible to do something It is possible to get there by bus.
  - It's just not physically possible to finish all this by the end of the week.
  - it is possible for somebody to do something Would it be possible for me to leave a message for her?

  - heart disease/problems

### 458. [wife](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/wife) *(noun)*

  - He is survived by his wife Anne.
  - She has a wife and two children.

<a id="w-459"></a>
### 459. [whole](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/whole_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/həʊl/` · **US:** `/həʊl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/who/whole/whole__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/who/whole/whole__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पूरा, तमाम, निःशेष
- **Examples:**

  - Jenna was my best friend in the whole world.
  - It seems I've spent my whole life travelling.
  - The whole family will be there.

<a id="w-460"></a>
### 460. [police](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/police_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pəˈliːs/` · **US:** `/pəˈliːs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pol/polic/police__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pol/polic/police__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - Contact the police if you see anything suspicious.
  - The police arrested three men and took them for questioning.
  - Police are investigating the break-in.

<a id="w-461"></a>
### 461. [mind](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/mind_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/maɪnd/` · **US:** `/maɪnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/min/mind_/mind__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/min/mind_/mind__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्मरण, ध्यान, मत
- **Examples:**
  - the conscious/subconscious mind
  - There were all kinds of thoughts running through my mind.
  - There was no doubt in his mind that he'd get the job.
  - ‘Drugs’ are associated in most people's minds with drug abuse.

<a id="w-462"></a>
### 462. [finally](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/finally) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfaɪnəli/` · **US:** `/ˈfaɪnəli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fin/final/finally__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fin/final/finally__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अंत में, आखिरकार, अंततोगत्वा
- **Examples:**
  - The performance finally started half an hour late.
  - The law was finally passed in May 2019.
  - I finally managed to get her attention.
  - Are you finally going to tell me why I’m here?

<a id="w-463"></a>
### 463. [pull](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/pull_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pʊl/` · **US:** `/pʊl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pul/pull_/pull__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pul/pull_/pull__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-464"></a>
### 464. [return](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/return_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈtɜːn/` · **US:** `/rɪˈtɜːrn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/ret/retur/return__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/ret/retur/return__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विवरण, लाभ, लौटती डाक से
- **Examples:**

  - return of somebody from… I was a bit bored after my return from holiday.
  - return of somebody to…from… On my return to England from Dublin my Mum picked me up at the airport.
  - She lives in fear of his return.

### 465. [free](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/free_1) *(adjective)*

- **Meaning:** खाली, स्वतंत्र, मुक्त करना
- **Examples:**

  - We try to keep Sundays free.
  - free for something If Sarah is free for lunch I'll take her out.
  - Keep Friday night free for my party.

- **Meaning:** विवरण, रिपोर्ट, जानकारी

- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/according)

- **UK:** `/dɪˈsɪʒn/` · **US:** `/dɪˈsɪʒn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dec/decis/decision__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dec/decis/decision__us_4.mp3)

## Words 501 – 600

<a id="w-501"></a>
### 501. [director](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/director) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/daɪˈrektə(r)/` · **US:** `/daɪˈrektər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dir/direc/director__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dir/direc/director__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रबंधक, संचालक, अध्यक्ष
- **Examples:**
  - a non-executive director
  - He's on the board of directors.

<a id="w-502"></a>
### 502. [position](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/position_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pəˈzɪʃn/` · **US:** `/pəˈzɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pos/posit/position__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pos/posit/position__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दृष्टिकोण, प्रतिष्ठा, पद
- **Examples:**
  - Where would be the best position for the lights?
  - from a/your position From his position on the cliff top, he had a good view of the harbour.
  - She looked down over the scene from a raised position.
  - in a position These plants will grow well in a sheltered position.

<a id="w-503"></a>

- **UK:** `/ˈpleɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈpleɪər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pla/playe/player__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pla/playe/player__us_2.mp3)

- **UK:** `/əˈɡriː/` · **US:** `/əˈɡriː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/agr/agree/agree__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/agr/agree/agree__us_1.mp3)

  - When he said that, I had to agree.

  - agree with somebody I totally agree with you!
  - agree about/on something You can't expect them to agree on everything.

<a id="w-505"></a>
### 505. [especially](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/especially) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈspeʃəli/` · **US:** `/ɪˈspeʃəli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/esp/espec/especially__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/esp/espec/especially__us_1_rr.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - Teenagers are very fashion conscious, especially girls.
  - especially + adv./prep. I love Rome, especially in the spring.
  - especially if… The car is quite small, especially if you have children.

<a id="w-506"></a>
### 506. [record](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/record_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈrekɔːd/` · **US:** `/ˈrekərd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rec/recor/record__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rec/recor/record__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/ˌsɪtʃuˈeɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌsɪtʃuˈeɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sit/situa/situation__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sit/situa/situation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समस्या, दृश्य, स्थिति
- **Examples:**
  - the present economic/financial/political situation
  - There is no doubt that the current situation is very serious.
  - People are fed up with the present situation.
  - in a situation We are now in a difficult situation.

### 537. [cost](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/cost_1) *(noun)*

  - To order, simply click here.

  - She says that the company was simply trying to protect its business.
  - Simply add hot water and stir.

  - evidence to suggest, support, show, etc. Have you any evidence to support this allegation?

- **UK:** `/ˈsʌmə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈsʌmər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sum/summe/summer__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sum/summe/summer__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ग्रीष्म ऋतु, गर्मी, ग्रीष्मकालीन
- **Examples:**
  - in the summer We're going away in the summer.
  - in the summer of 2019
  - this/next/last summer
  - two summers ago

<a id="w-599"></a>
### 599. [less](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/less_3) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/les/` · **US:** `/les/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/les/less_/less__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/les/less_/less__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** छोटा, कम माऋआ मे़, कम
- **Examples:**
  - less expensive/likely/intelligent
  - less often/enthusiastically
  - less than… I read much less now than I used to.
  - The receptionist was less than (= not at all) helpful.

<a id="w-600"></a>
### 600. [realize](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/realize) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈrɪəlaɪz/` · **US:** `/ˈriːəlaɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rea/reali/realize__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rea/reali/realize__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्राप्त करना, समझना, रूपये में परिवर्तित करना
- **Examples:**
  - realize (that)… I didn’t realize (that) you were so unhappy.
  - The moment I saw her, I realized something was wrong.
  - I finally came to realize that he would never change.
  - It is important to realize that there are still potential problems.

## Words 601 – 700

<a id="w-601"></a>
### 601. [hundred](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hundred) *(number)*
- **UK:** `/ˈhʌndrəd/` · **US:** `/ˈhʌndrəd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hun/hundr/hundred__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hun/hundr/hundred__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सौ, सौ/शत, सैकड़ा
- **Examples:**
  - One hundred (of the children) have already been placed with foster families.
  - There were just a hundred of them there.
  - This vase is worth several hundred dollars.
  - She must be over a hundred (= a hundred years old).

<a id="w-602"></a>
### 602. [available](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/available) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əˈveɪləbl/` · **US:** `/əˈveɪləbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ava/avail/available__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ava/avail/available__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - available resources/data

  - Further details are available on request.
  - We'll send you a copy as soon as it becomes available.

### 605. [opportunity](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/opportunity_1) *(noun)*

<a id="w-630"></a>
### 630. [risk](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/risk_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rɪsk/` · **US:** `/rɪsk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/ris/risk_/risk__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/ris/risk_/risk__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जोखिम
- **Examples:**
  - The health risks are very low.
  - risk of something a high risk of failure
  - a serious/significant risk of something
  - The study found a slightly increased risk of cancer in this group.

<a id="w-631"></a>
### 631. [current](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/current_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkʌrənt/` · **US:** `/ˈkɜːrənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cur/curre/current__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cur/curre/current__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वर्तमानकालिक, अग
- **Examples:**
  - The necklace would be worth over $5 000 at current prices.
  - the current situation
  - Oil prices are expected to remain at current levels.
  - What's the budget for the current year?

<a id="w-632"></a>
### 632. [fire](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/fire_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfaɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈfaɪər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fir/fire_/fire__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fir/fire_/fire__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** गुस्सा, विपत्ति, जोश

  - When did people start to cook with fire?

<a id="w-633"></a>
### 633. [future](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/future_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfjuːtʃə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈfjuːtʃər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fut/futur/future__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fut/futur/future__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भविष्य, अवैया
- **Examples:**
  - The land will remain untouched for future generations to enjoy.
  - The reasons for the decision will be announced at a future date.
  - future developments in artificial intelligence
  - present and future trends in industry

<a id="w-634"></a>
### 634. [wrong](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/wrong_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/rɒŋ/` · **US:** `/rɔːŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wro/wrong/wrong__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wro/wrong/wrong__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** गलत, असत्य
- **Examples:**
  - I got all the answers wrong.
  - He was driving on the wrong side of the road.
  - Sorry, I must have dialled the wrong number.
  - You're holding the camera the wrong way up!

<a id="w-635"></a>
### 635. [involve](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/involve) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈvɒlv/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈvɑːlv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/invol/involve__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/invol/involve__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शामिल होना, शामिल करना, खतरे में पडना
- **Examples:**
  - involve something Any investment involves an element of risk.
  - involve doing something The process involves using steam to sterilize the instruments.
  - The test will involve answering questions about a photograph.
  - involve somebody/something doing something The job involves me travelling all over the country.

<a id="w-636"></a>
### 636. [defense](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/defence) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈfens/` · **US:** `/dɪˈfens/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/def/defen/defence__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/def/defen/defence__us_12.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बचाव, सुरक्षा, रक्षा
- **Examples:**
  - in defence of somebody/something soldiers who died in defence of their country
  - What points can be raised in defence of this argument?
  - in somebody's defence I have to say in her defence that she knew nothing about it beforehand.
  - When her brother was criticized she leapt to his defence.

<a id="w-637"></a>
### 637. [anyone](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/anyone) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈeniwʌn/` · **US:** `/ˈeniwʌn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/any/anyon/anyone__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/any/anyon/anyone__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** किसी को, कोई भी, कोई  भी
- **Examples:**
  - Is anyone there?
  - Does anyone else want to come?
  - Did anyone see you?
  - Hardly anyone came.

<a id="w-638"></a>
### 638. [increase](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/increase_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪŋkriːs/` · **US:** `/ˈɪŋkriːs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inc/incre/increase__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inc/incre/increase__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विस्तार, प्रगति, वृद्धि
- **Examples:**
  - price/tax/wage increases
  - Profits show a steady increase.
  - increase in something Last year the business saw a 3% increase in turnover.
  - a substantial increase in the number of products on the market

<a id="w-639"></a>
### 639. [security](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/security) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/sɪˈkjʊərəti/` · **US:** `/sɪˈkjʊrəti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sec/secur/security__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sec/secur/security__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सुरक्षा, सुरक्षा उपाय, सुरक्षा  उपाय
- **Examples:**
  - national/homeland security (= the defence of a country)
  - airport security officers
  - security against something The bars are to provide security against break-ins.
  - They carried out security checks at the airport.

<a id="w-640"></a>
### 640. [bank](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/bank_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/bæŋk/` · **US:** `/bæŋk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/ban/bank_/bank__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/ban/bank_/bank__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** किनारा, रेती, ढेर
- **Examples:**

  - I need to go to the bank (= the local office of a bank).
  - We are now in competition with the big foreign banks.
  - Major banks are increasing the amount they lend to small companies.

<a id="w-641"></a>
### 641. [myself](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/myself) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/maɪˈself/` · **US:** `/maɪˈself/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mys/mysel/myself__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mys/mysel/myself__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्वतः
- **Examples:**
  - I cut myself on a knife.
  - I wrote a message to myself.
  - I found myself unable to speak.
  - I haven't been feeling myself recently (= I have not felt well).

<a id="w-642"></a>
### 642. [certainly](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/certainly) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsɜːtnli/` · **US:** `/ˈsɜːrtnli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cer/certa/certainly__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cer/certa/certainly__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निश्चित रूप से, निःसन्देह, अवश्य
- **Examples:**
  - Without treatment, she will almost certainly die.
  - Certainly, the early years are crucial to a child's development.
  - I'm certainly never going there again.
  - I certainly hope we can do this again.

### 643. [west](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/west_2) *(adjective)*

  - on the west side of the building
  - Australia's west coast

- **UK:** `/spɔːt/` · **US:** `/spɔːrt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/spo/sport/sport__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/spo/sport/sport__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/bɔːd/` · **US:** `/bɔːrd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/boa/board/board__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/boa/board/board__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-646"></a>
### 646. [seek](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/seek) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/siːk/` · **US:** `/siːk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/see/seek_/seek__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/see/seek_/seek__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - Police are not seeking anyone else in connection with the deaths.
  - seek for something/somebody (British English) They sought in vain for somewhere to shelter.

<a id="w-647"></a>
### 647. [per](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/per) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/pɜː(r)/` · **US:** `/pɜːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/per__/per__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/per__/per__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रति
- **Examples:**
  - Rooms cost £50 per person, per night.
  - 60 miles per hour
  - France consumes around 200 000 tonnes of cocoa per year.
  - This country has a higher crime rate per 100 000 of the population than most other European countries.

<a id="w-648"></a>
### 648. [subject](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/subject_2) *(adjective)*

- **Examples:**

  - The rent is subject to review every five years.
  - Flights are subject to delay because of the fog.

### 649. [officer](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/officer) *(noun)*

  - He's a retired army intelligence officer.

### 650. [private](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/private_1) *(adjective)*

- **Examples:**

  - a private jet
  - Those are my father's private papers.

<a id="w-651"></a>
### 651. [rest](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/rest_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rest/` · **US:** `/rest/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/rest_/rest__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/rest_/rest__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्थिर, विराम, ठहराव

  - the rest of something The country enjoys friendly relations with the rest of the world.
  - How would you like to spend the rest of the day?
  - for the rest of something I'm not doing this job for the rest of my life.

<a id="w-652"></a>
### 652. [behavior](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/behaviour) *(noun)*

  - good/bad behaviour
  - social/sexual/criminal behaviour
  - a plan to tackle antisocial behaviour
  - behaviour towards somebody His behaviour towards her was becoming more and more aggressive.

<a id="w-653"></a>
### 653. [deal](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/deal_2) *(noun)*

  - a deal with somebody We did a deal with the management on overtime.

### 654. [performance](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/performance) *(noun)*

### 655. [fight](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/fight_2) *(noun)*

### 656. [throw](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/throw_1) *(verb)*

<a id="w-657"></a>
### 657. [top](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/top_2) *(adjective)*

- **Meaning:** मुख्य, सर्वोच्च स्थान

  - She kept her passport in the top drawer.

<a id="w-658"></a>
### 658. [quickly](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/quickly) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkwɪkli/` · **US:** `/ˈkwɪkli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/q/qui/quick/quickly__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/q/qui/quick/quickly__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तुरंत, शीघ्रता से, सरसरी तौर पर
- **Examples:**

  - The disease spreads quickly.

### 659. [past](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/past_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/pɑːst/` · **US:** `/pæst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pas/past_/past__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pas/past_/past__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भूत
- **Examples:**
  - in past years/centuries
  - in times past

  - The days are long past when people worked at the same company all their lives.

<a id="w-660"></a>
### 660. [goal](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/goal) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡəʊl/` · **US:** `/ɡəʊl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/goa/goal_/goal__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/goa/goal_/goal__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - He headed the ball into an open goal (= one that had nobody defending it).
  - in goal Nicky Roberts was outstanding in goal (= as goalkeeper).
  - The goalkeeper was injured so a defender had to go in goal.

<a id="w-661"></a>
### 661. [second](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/second1_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsekənd/` · **US:** `/ˈsekənd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sec/secon/second__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sec/secon/second__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्थानान्तरित करना, सहारा देना, नियुक्त करना
- **Examples:**
  - She came second in the marathon.
  - One of the smaller parties came a close second (= nearly won).
  - The women's team came in fourth and the men finished second.
  - I agreed to speak second.

<a id="w-662"></a>
### 662. [bed](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/bed_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/bed/` · **US:** `/bed/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bed/bed__/bed__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bed/bed__/bed__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आधार, सेज, तल
- **Examples:**
  - a single/double bed
  - on the bed She lay on the bed (= on top of the covers).
  - in bed He lay in bed (= under the covers).
  - I'm tired—I'm going to bed.

<a id="w-663"></a>
### 663. [order](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/order_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɔːdə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈɔːrdər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/ord/order/order__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/ord/order/order__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वर्ग, क्रम, व्यवस्था
- **Examples:**
  - in… order The names are listed in alphabetical order.
  - The winning scores, in reverse order, are as follows.
  - in the/a… order Put the words in the correct order.
  - Let's take the problems in a different order.

<a id="w-664"></a>
### 664. [author](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/author_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɔːθə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈɔːθər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/aut/autho/author__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/aut/autho/author__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जड़, प्रवर्तक, लेखक
- **Examples:**
  - Who is your favourite author?
  - He is the author of three books on art.
  - best-selling author Paul Theroux
  - the lead author of the report

<a id="w-665"></a>
### 665. [fill](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/fill_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/fɪl/` · **US:** `/fɪl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fil/fill_/fill__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fil/fill_/fill__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बताना, पूरा  करना, खाना
- **Examples:**
  - fill something Please fill this glass for me.
  - to fill a space/vacuum/void
  - Smoke filled the room.
  - Her image filled the screen.

<a id="w-666"></a>
### 666. [represent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/represent) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˌreprɪˈzent/` · **US:** `/ˌreprɪˈzent/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rep/repre/represent__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rep/repre/represent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रदर्शन करना, प्रस्तुत करना, रखना
- **Examples:**
  - The competition attracted over 500 contestants representing eight different countries.
  - Local businesses are well represented on the committee (= there are a lot of people from them on the committee).
  - The opening speech was by Bob Alan representing Amnesty International.
  - The President was represented at the ceremony by the Vice-President.

<a id="w-667"></a>
### 667. [focus](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/focus_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfəʊkəs/` · **US:** `/ˈfəʊkəs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/foc/focus/focus__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/foc/focus/focus__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मुद्दा, केंद्र, केंद्रबिन्दु
- **Examples:**
  - focus of something It was the main focus of attention at the meeting.
  - The primary focus of the book is practical teaching techniques.
  - He decided to make colour the central focus of his work.
  - focus for something His comments provided a focus for debate.

<a id="w-668"></a>
### 668. [foreign](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/foreign) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfɒrən/` · **US:** `/ˈfɔːrən/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/for/forei/foreign__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/for/forei/foreign__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भिन्न, बाह्री, परदेशी
- **Examples:**
  - What foreign languages do you speak?
  - It can be a challenge at first living in a foreign country.
  - I went to the bank to get some foreign currency.
  - a foreign-owned company

<a id="w-669"></a>
### 669. [drop](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/drop_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/drɒp/` · **US:** `/drɑːp/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dro/drop_/drop__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dro/drop_/drop__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** गिरावट, झुमका, ड्राप
- **Examples:**
  - The first drops of rain fell.
  - There was a drop of blood on his finger.
  - a drop of water/oil/sweat
  - Mix a few drops of milk into the cake mixture.

<a id="w-670"></a>
### 670. [plan](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/plan_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/plæn/` · **US:** `/plæn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pla/plan_/plan__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pla/plan_/plan__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नक़्शा, व्यवस्था, मापचित्र
- **Examples:**
  - plan for something Do you have any plans for the summer?
  - plan to do something There are no plans to build new offices.
  - Your best plan (= the best thing to do) would be to go by car.
  - There's been a change of plan.

<a id="w-671"></a>
### 671. [blood](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/blood_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/blʌd/` · **US:** `/blʌd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/blo/blood/blood__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/blo/blood/blood__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जोश, बलि, स्वभाव
- **Examples:**
  - He lost a lot of blood in the accident.
  - Blood was pouring out of a cut on her head.
  - His hands were covered in blood.
  - to give blood (= to have blood taken from you so that it can be used in the medical treatment of other people)

<a id="w-672"></a>
### 672. [upon](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/upon) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/əˈpɒn/` · **US:** `/əˈpɑːn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/u/upo/upon_/upon__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/u/upo/upon_/upon__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नज़दीक, के ऊपर/ऊँचा/पर
- **Examples:**

<a id="w-673"></a>
### 673. [agency](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/agency) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈeɪdʒənsi/` · **US:** `/ˈeɪdʒənsi/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/age/agenc/agency__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/age/agenc/agency__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** माध्यम, संस्था, संस्था {कर्तृत्व}
- **Examples:**
  - She works for an advertising agency.
  - through an agency He managed to find a job through an agency.

<a id="w-674"></a>
### 674. [push](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/push_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pʊʃ/` · **US:** `/pʊʃ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pus/push_/push__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pus/push_/push__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दबाव, धक्का, पूरी कोशिश
- **Examples:**
  - She gave him a gentle push.
  - The car won't start. Can you give it a push?
  - (figurative) At the push of a button (= very easily) he could get a whole list of names.

<a id="w-675"></a>
### 675. [nature](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/nature_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈneɪtʃə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈneɪtʃər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nat/natur/nature__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nat/natur/nature__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रकार, गुण, वर्ग
- **Examples:**
  - Take time to appreciate the beauties of nature.
  - man-made substances not found in nature
  - A lot of nature conservation is about protecting habitats.

<a id="w-676"></a>
### 676. [color](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/colour_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkʌlə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈkʌlər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/col/colou/colour__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/col/colou/colour__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जीवंतता
- **Examples:**
  - What's your favourite colour?
  - bright/vibrant/bold colours
  - She always wears dark colours.
  - I'd like to paint this room in a lighter colour.

<a id="w-677"></a>
### 677. [no](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/no_2) *(determiner)*
- **UK:** `/nəʊ/` · **US:** `/nəʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/no_/no__g/no__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/no_/no__u/no__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कोई, नहीं, बिल्कुल नहीं
- **Examples:**
  - No student is to leave the room.
  - I have no meetings this morning.
  - There's no bread left.
  - No two days are the same.

<a id="w-678"></a>
### 678. [recently](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/recently) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈriːsntli/` · **US:** `/ˈriːsntli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rec/recen/recently__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rec/recen/recently__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हाल ही में, अभी अभी, हाल मेँ
- **Examples:**
  - a recently published book
  - a recently released report
  - The company recently announced plans to lay off one-fifth of its workforce.
  - She recently completed a follow-up study of participants.

<a id="w-679"></a>
### 679. [store](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/store_1) *(noun)*

- **Meaning:** गोदाम, स्टोर, संचय
- **Examples:**
  - It's available at London's three biggest stores.

<a id="w-680"></a>
### 680. [reduce](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/reduce) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈdjuːs/` · **US:** `/rɪˈduːs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/red/reduc/reduce__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/red/reduc/reduce__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** घटना, कम करना, पराजित करना
- **Examples:**
  - reduce something Reduce speed now (= on a sign).
  - Giving up smoking reduces the risk of heart disease.
  - to reduce costs/numbers
  - a reduced rate/price

<a id="w-681"></a>
### 681. [sound](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/sound_3) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/saʊnd/` · **US:** `/saʊnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sou/sound/sound__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sou/sound/sound__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** युक्तियुक्त, नाद
- **Examples:**
  - He's a person of very sound judgement.
  - He gave me some very sound advice.
  - This gives the design team a sound basis for their work.
  - The proposal makes sound commercial sense.

<a id="w-682"></a>
### 682. [note](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/note_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/nəʊt/` · **US:** `/nəʊt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/not/note_/note__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/not/note_/note__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ध्यान, कीर्ति, स्वर
- **Examples:**
  - Please make a note of the dates.
  - She made a mental note (= decided that she must remember) to ask Alan about it.
  - Note to self (= a mental instruction): book more holidays.

<a id="w-683"></a>
### 683. [fine](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/fine_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/faɪn/` · **US:** `/faɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fin/fine_/fine__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fin/fine_/fine__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सुन्दर, संतोषजनक, शुद्ध
- **Examples:**
  - ‘How are you?’ ‘Fine, thanks.’
  - I was feeling fine when I got up this morning.
  - ‘How’s your throat?’ ‘It’s fine as long as I don’t cough.’

<a id="w-684"></a>
### 684. [near](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/near_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/nɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/nɪr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nea/near_/near__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nea/near_/near__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निकट, तटस्थ
- **Examples:**
  - His house is very near.
  - Where's the nearest bank?
  - Wales may be a near neighbour but its history is quite distinct.

<a id="w-685"></a>
### 685. [movement](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/movement) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈmuːvmənt/` · **US:** `/ˈmuːvmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mov/movem/movement__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mov/movem/movement__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लय, झुकाव, गति
- **Examples:**
  - hand/eye movements
  - She observed the gentle movement of his chest as he breathed.
  - Loose clothing gives you greater freedom of movement.
  - Power comes from the forward movement of the entire body.

<a id="w-686"></a>
### 686. [page](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/page_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/peɪdʒ/` · **US:** `/peɪdʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pag/page_/page__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pag/page_/page__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पृष्ठ, सामानवाही नौकर, पेज
- **Examples:**
  - Turn to page 64.
  - Someone has torn a page out of this book.
  - She slowly turned the pages of the album.
  - a blank page

<a id="w-687"></a>
### 687. [enter](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/enter) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈentə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈentər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/ent/enter/enter__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/ent/enter/enter__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भर्ति करना, डाला जाना, जाना
- **Examples:**
  - Knock before you enter.
  - Enter Hamlet (= for example as stage directions in the text of a play).
  - enter something Someone entered the room behind me.
  - people who enter the country illegally

<a id="w-688"></a>
### 688. [share](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/share_3) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ʃeə(r)/` · **US:** `/ʃer/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sha/share/share__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sha/share/share__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हिस्सा, अंश, अंश विभाजन
- **Examples:**
  - share of something Next year we hope to have a greater share of the market.
  - The charity was supposed to have been given a share of the profits.
  - The party won its biggest share of the vote since 1992.
  - The new TV channel has continued to increase its share of advertising revenue.

<a id="w-689"></a>
### 689. [common](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/common_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒmən/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːmən/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/commo/common__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/commo/common__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** साधारण, सार्वजनिक
- **Examples:**
  - a common problem/occurrence/practice
  - a common feature/theme
  - the most common type of injury
  - Breast cancer is the most common form of cancer among women in this country.

<a id="w-690"></a>
### 690. [poor](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/poor) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/pʊə(r)/` · **US:** `/pɔːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/poo/poor_/poor__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/poo/poor_/poor__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** असहाय, निःस्व
- **Examples:**
  - They were too poor to buy shoes for the kids.
  - We aim to help the poorest families.
  - It's among the poorer countries of the world.
  - This is a relatively poor neighbourhood.

<a id="w-691"></a>
### 691. [other](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/other_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈʌðə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈʌðər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/oth/other/other__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/oth/other/other__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दुसरा, अन्य, दूसरा
- **Examples:**
  - Mr Harris and Mrs Bate and three other teachers were there.
  - Are there any other questions?
  - There seem to be no other survivors.
  - I can't see you now—some other time, maybe.

<a id="w-692"></a>
### 692. [natural](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/natural_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnætʃrəl/` · **US:** `/ˈnætʃrəl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nat/natur/natural__gb_6.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nat/natur/natural__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्वाभाविक
- **Examples:**
  - the natural world (= of trees, rivers, animals and birds)
  - We should respect animals and the natural environment, which is their home.
  - a country’s natural resources (= its coal, oil, forests, etc.)
  - wildlife in its natural habitat

### 701. [usually](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/usually) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈjuːʒəli/` · **US:** `/ˈjuːʒəli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/u/usu/usual/usually__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/u/usu/usual/usually__us_1_rr.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - How long does the journey usually take?
  - Tension headache is not usually associated with any other symptoms.
  - For Americans, ‘mad’ usually means ‘angry’.
  - Usually, there's no extra charge for delivery.

- **UK:** `/ˈhæpi/` · **US:** `/ˈhæpi/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hap/happy/happy__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hap/happy/happy__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आनन्दित, लापरवाह, प्रसन्न
- **Examples:**
  - a happy family
  - a happy smile/face
  - You don't look very happy today.
  - He always seemed happy enough.

<a id="w-725"></a>
### 725. [exactly](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/exactly) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪɡˈzæktli/` · **US:** `/ɪɡˈzæktli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/exa/exact/exactly__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/exa/exact/exactly__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वास्तव में, ठीक ठीक/यथार्थतः, वास्तव  में
- **Examples:**
  - It's exactly nine o'clock.
  - It happened almost exactly a year ago.
  - Your answer is exactly right.
  - It was a warm day, if not exactly hot.

<a id="w-726"></a>
### 726. [protect](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/protect) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/prəˈtekt/` · **US:** `/prəˈtekt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pro/prote/protect__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pro/prote/protect__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बचाना, सुरक्षित  करना, रक्षा करना
- **Examples:**
  - protect somebody/something/yourself to protect children/citizens/civilians/consumers/workers
  - Our aim is to protect the jobs of our members.
  - the section of the treaty that protects the rights of employees
  - Each company is fighting to protect its own commercial interests.

<a id="w-727"></a>
### 727. [approach](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/approach_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈprəʊtʃ/` · **US:** `/əˈprəʊtʃ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/app/appro/approach__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/app/appro/approach__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पद्धति, रास्ता, प्रस्ताव
- **Examples:**
  - She took the wrong approach in her dealings with them.
  - It was time to take a different approach.
  - an alternative/innovative approach
  - a traditional/similar approach

### 728. [lie](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/lie2_2) *(noun)*

### 734. [media](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/media) *(noun)*

  - print/electronic/broadcast media

<a id="w-735"></a>
### 735. [ready](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ready_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈredi/` · **US:** `/ˈredi/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rea/ready/ready__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rea/ready/ready__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - Just a minute—I'm almost ready.
  - Are you nearly ready?
  - ‘Shall we go?’ ‘I'm ready when you are!’
  - ready for something I'm just getting the kids ready for school.

- **UK:** `/saɪn/` · **US:** `/saɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sig/sign_/sign__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sig/sign_/sign__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संकेत, चिन्ह, लक्षण
- **Examples:**
