  - a clear/visible sign
  - an encouraging sign
  - a positive sign

<a id="w-737"></a>
### 737. [thought](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/thought) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/θɔːt/` · **US:** `/θɔːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tho/thoug/thought__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tho/thoug/thought__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विचार, मत, इरादा
- **Examples:**
  - thought of doing something I couldn't bear the thought of waiting any longer.
  - thought of something/somebody The very thought of it makes me feel sick.
  - thought of somebody/something doing something I don't like the thought of you walking home alone.
  - thought (that)… She was struck by the sudden thought that he might already have left.

<a id="w-738"></a>
### 738. [list](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/list_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/lɪst/` · **US:** `/lɪst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lis/list_/list__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lis/list_/list__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मेंड़, तालिका, प्ंअक्ति
- **Examples:**
  - The guest list includes numerous celebrities.
  - on the list Is your name on the list?
  - I'll add you to the email list.
  - Her novel shot to the top of the bestseller list.

<a id="w-739"></a>
### 739. [individual](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/individual_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪndɪˈvɪdʒuəl/` · **US:** `/ˌɪndɪˈvɪdʒuəl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ind/indiv/individual__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ind/indiv/individual__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विशिष्ट, व्यक्ति, अलग अलग
- **Examples:**
  - We interviewed each individual member of the community.
  - The minister refused to comment on individual cases.
  - Farmers each have their own individual concerns about the changes.
  - The individual members of the team must all be motivated to contribute.

<a id="w-740"></a>
### 740. [simple](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/simple) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsɪmpl/` · **US:** `/ˈsɪmpl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sim/simpl/simple__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sim/simpl/simple__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सरल, निष्कपट, सादा
- **Examples:**
  - a simple solution/explanation/question/task/example
  - simple for somebody/something You will soon see that what once seemed impossible is now simple for you.
  - something is simple to do This machine is very simple to use.
  - something is simple for somebody to do The game is supposed to be simple for anyone to play.

<a id="w-741"></a>
### 741. [quality](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/quality_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkwɒləti/` · **US:** `/ˈkwɑːləti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/q/qua/quali/quality__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/q/qua/quali/quality__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रकार, कोटि, गुण
- **Examples:**
  - Improving the quality of care for nursing home residents is a priority.
  - When costs are cut product quality suffers.
  - of… quality The soil here is of poor quality.
  - The food is fresh and of good quality.

<a id="w-742"></a>
### 742. [pressure](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/pressure_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpreʃə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈpreʃər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/press/pressure__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/press/pressure__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बल, प्रभाव, दबाव
- **Examples:**
  - pressure for something The pressure for change continued to mount.
  - pressure on somebody We are determined to keep up the pressure on the other member states.
  - pressure on somebody to do something There is intense pressure on her to resign.
  - pressure to do something In every society there is strong pressure to conform.

<a id="w-743"></a>
### 743. [accept](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/accept) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əkˈsept/` · **US:** `/əkˈsept/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acc/accep/accept__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acc/accep/accept__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** करना, स्वीकार करना, के साथ मैथुन के लिये तैयार होना
- **Examples:**
  - He asked me to marry him and I accepted.
  - accept something Please accept our sincere apologies.
  - It was pouring with rain so I accepted his offer of a lift.
  - I am unfortunately unable to accept your kind invitation.

<a id="w-744"></a>
### 744. [answer](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/answer_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɑːnsə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈænsər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ans/answe/answer__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ans/answe/answer__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हल, उत्तर, जवाब
- **Examples:**
  - I rang the bell, but there was no answer.
  - Can't you give me a definitive answer?
  - The short answer is no.
  - The simple answer is that we don't know.

<a id="w-745"></a>
### 745. [hard](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hard_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/hɑːd/` · **US:** `/hɑːrd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/har/hard_/hard__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/har/hard_/hard__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अटल, कटु, नृशंस
- **Examples:**
  - a hard choice/decision/question
  - hard to do something It is hard to believe that she's only nine.
  - It's hard to see how they can lose.
  - It's hard to imagine a more cynical political strategy.

<a id="w-746"></a>
### 746. [resource](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/resource_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈzɔːs/` · **US:** `/rɪˈsɔːrs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resou/resource__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resou/resource__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सहारा, स्रोत, साधन
- **Examples:**
  - the exploitation of minerals and other natural resources
  - They promote the use of renewable resources like soybean oil.
  - water/mineral/energy resources
  - The school does the best it can with limited resources (= money).

<a id="w-747"></a>
### 747. [identify](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/identify) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/aɪˈdentɪfaɪ/` · **US:** `/aɪˈdentɪfaɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ide/ident/identify__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ide/ident/identify__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समझना, पहचानना, शिनाख्त  करना
- **Examples:**
  - identify somebody/something She was able to identify her attacker.
  - Passengers were asked to identify their own suitcases before they were put on the plane.
  - identify yourself Many of those arrested refused to identify themselves (= would not say who they were).
  - identify somebody/something as somebody/something The bodies were identified as those of two suspected drug dealers.

<a id="w-748"></a>
### 748. [left](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/left_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/left/` · **US:** `/left/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lef/left_/left__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lef/left_/left__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बाँया, वाम पंथ
- **Examples:**
  - Fewer people write with their left hand than with their right.
  - your left knee/leg/foot/arm
  - the left side of the field
  - The university is on the left bank of the river.

<a id="w-749"></a>
### 749. [meeting](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/meeting) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈmiːtɪŋ/` · **US:** `/ˈmiːtɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mee/meeti/meeting__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mee/meeti/meeting__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बैठक, सभा, मीटिंग
- **Examples:**
  - A hundred people attended the public meeting.
  - The meeting will be held in the school hall.
  - to schedule/convene/arrange/organize/call a meeting
  - Helen will chair the meeting (= be in charge of it).

<a id="w-750"></a>
### 750. [determine](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/determine) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈtɜːmɪn/` · **US:** `/dɪˈtɜːrmɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/det/deter/determine__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/det/deter/determine__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निश्चित करना, तय करना, निर्धारित करना
- **Examples:**
  - determine something An inquiry was set up to determine the cause of the accident.
  - Try to determine the extent of the problem.
  - determine what, whether, etc… We set out to determine exactly what happened that night.
  - It is difficult to determine when drama first appeared in India.

<a id="w-751"></a>
### 751. [prepare](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/prepare) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈpeə(r)/` · **US:** `/prɪˈper/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prepa/prepare__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prepa/prepare__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रस्तूत होना, तैयार करना, प्रस्तुत करना
- **Examples:**
  - prepare something/somebody to prepare a report
  - prepare something/somebody for somebody/something A hotel room is being prepared for them.
  - The college prepares students for a career in business.
  - prepare somebody/something to do something The training has prepared me to deal with any medical issue.

<a id="w-752"></a>
### 752. [disease](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/disease) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈziːz/` · **US:** `/dɪˈziːz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dis/disea/disease__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dis/disea/disease__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** रोग, बीमारी, व्याधि
- **Examples:**
  - heart/lung/liver disease
  - In 19th century England, infectious diseases were the principal cause of death.
  - chronic diseases such as obesity and diabetes
  - health measures to prevent the spread of disease

<a id="w-753"></a>
### 753. [whatever](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/whatever_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/wɒtˈevə(r)/` · **US:** `/wətˈevər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wha/whate/whatever__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wha/whate/whatever__us_3_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कुछ भी, जो भी, जो कुछ भी
- **Examples:**
  - They received no help whatever.
  - ‘Is there any doubt about it?’ ‘None whatsoever.’

<a id="w-754"></a>
### 754. [success](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/success) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/səkˈses/` · **US:** `/səkˈses/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/suc/succe/success__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/suc/succe/success__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सफल व्यक्ति, सौभाग्य, सफलता
- **Examples:**
  - The company has achieved considerable success in this market.
  - Initially the venture enjoyed a fair amount of success.
  - success in doing something I didn't have much success in finding a job.
  - without success I tried a second time, but without success.

<a id="w-785"></a>
### 785. [message](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/message_1) *(noun)*

- **Examples:**

  - I never got your message.
  - We've had an urgent message saying that your father's ill.
  - Jenny's not here at the moment. Can I take a message?

### 786. [fail](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/fail_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/feɪl/` · **US:** `/feɪl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fai/fail_/fail__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fai/fail_/fail__us_1.mp3)

### 806. [crime](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/crime) *(noun)*

- **UK:** `/kəmˈpeə(r)/` · **US:** `/kəmˈper/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/compa/compare__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/compa/compare__us_2.mp3)

### 811. [miss](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/miss_1) *(verb)*

<a id="w-813"></a>

<a id="w-849"></a>
### 849. [forget](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/forget) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/fəˈɡet/` · **US:** `/fərˈɡet/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/for/forge/forget__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/for/forge/forget__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भूल जाना, भूलना, भूला देना
- **Examples:**
  - ‘Why weren't you at the meeting?’ ‘Sorry—I forgot.’
  - forget about something In the excitement I forgot all about my little brother.
  - forget to do something Take care, and don't forget to write.
  - He forgot to mention he was bringing a guest.

<a id="w-850"></a>
### 850. [since](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/since_3) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/sɪns/` · **US:** `/sɪns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sin/since/since__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sin/since/since__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तब  से, से, क्योंकि
- **Examples:**
  - She went for a run on Monday and has not been seen since.
  - She moved to Pakistan in 1997 and has been living in Lahore ever since.
  - He left home two weeks ago and we haven't heard from him since.
  - The health of the British people was better at the end of the Second World War than at any time before or since.

<a id="w-851"></a>
### 851. [claim](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/claim_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kleɪm/` · **US:** `/kleɪm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cla/claim/claim__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cla/claim/claim__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अधिकार, दावा, दावाआ
- **Examples:**
  - claim about somebody/something The company had made false claims about its products.
  - claim of something to reject/dismiss/deny claims of discrimination
  - a report examining claims of corrupt links between politicians
  - claim that… The singer has denied the magazine's claim that she is leaving the band.

<a id="w-852"></a>
### 852. [note](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/note_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/nəʊt/` · **US:** `/nəʊt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/not/note_/note__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/not/note_/note__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ध्यान, कीर्ति, स्वर
- **Examples:**
  - Please make a note of the dates.
  - She made a mental note (= decided that she must remember) to ask Alan about it.
  - Note to self (= a mental instruction): book more holidays.

<a id="w-853"></a>
### 853. [remove](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/remove_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈmuːv/` · **US:** `/rɪˈmuːv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rem/remov/remove__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rem/remov/remove__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निकालना, हटाना, अलग करना
- **Examples:**
  - remove somebody/something Illegally parked vehicles will be removed.
  - remove somebody/something from somebody/something He removed his hand from her shoulder.
  - Three children were removed from the school for persistent bad behaviour.
  - Remove the pan from the heat and continue to stir the sauce.

### 856. [close](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/close2_1) *(adjective)*

<a id="w-857"></a>
### 857. [sound](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/sound_3) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/saʊnd/` · **US:** `/saʊnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sou/sound/sound__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sou/sound/sound__us_1.mp3)

### 858. [enjoy](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/enjoy) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈdʒɔɪ/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈdʒɔɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/enj/enjoy/enjoy__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/enj/enjoy/enjoy__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - She seems to be enjoying life in Paris.
  - enjoy doing something I enjoy playing tennis and squash.

### 884. [avoid](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/avoid) *(verb)*

  - They narrowly avoided defeat.
  - The name was changed to avoid confusion with another firm.

<a id="w-885"></a>
### 885. [imagine](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/imagine) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈmædʒɪn/` · **US:** `/ɪˈmædʒɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ima/imagi/imagine__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ima/imagi/imagine__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - imagine something The house was just as she had imagined it.
  - Imagine my surprise when I opened the door to find him standing there.
  - I can't imagine life without the children now.
  - It's hard to imagine a more cynical political strategy.

<a id="w-886"></a>
### 886. [tonight](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/tonight_1) *(adverb)*

<a id="w-914"></a>
### 914. [pressure](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/pressure_1) *(noun)*

  - pressure to do something In every society there is strong pressure to conform.

<a id="w-975"></a>
### 975. [spring](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/spring_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/sprɪŋ/` · **US:** `/sprɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/spr/sprin/spring__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/spr/sprin/spring__us_1.mp3)

  - The birds arrive in late spring and leave again in early autumn.
  - He was born in the spring of 1944.
